Representative Vice Chairperson of the Committee on Appropriations, Monique Lagdameo,

**[00:24:17.040] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-000122)  
Good morning, Madam Speaker and distinguished members of this August Chamber.

**[00:24:21.620] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-000123)  
We are honored to sponsor today the proposed budget of the Metropolitan Manila

**[00:24:27.360] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-000124)  
Development Authority, or the MMDA, for the fiscal year 2027.

**[00:24:32.380] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-000125)  
The State recognizes Metro Manila as a special development and administrative region.

**[00:24:37.800] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-000126)  
By reason of the complex and interrelated challenges of rapid urbanization in Metro Manila,

**[00:24:43.280] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-000127)  
such as those relating to traffic management, solid waste disposal, flood mitigation and sewerage management,

**[00:24:49.900] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-000128)  
urban renewal, health and sanitation, and public safety, the MMDA was established under RA No. 7924,

**[00:24:57.360] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-000129)  
with a mandate, among other things, to formulate, coordinate, regulate, and monitor the implementation of medium and long-term plans and programs for the delivery of Metro-wide services.

**[00:25:09.600] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-000130)  
It works in close partnership with the 17 local governments to develop and implement integrated, sustainable, and resilient metropolitan development strategies for Metro Manila.

**[00:25:20.240] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-000131)  
Under House Bill No. 10858, or General Appropriations Bill for Fiscal Year 2027,

**[00:25:27.360] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-000132)  
the MMDA seeks the approval of its proposed national subsidy in the amount of Php 6,560,912,000.

**[00:25:35.440] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-000133)  
This allocation will provide the MMDA with the means and resources necessary to fulfill its mandate

**[00:25:41.200] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-000134)  
and fund and implement its core programs on solid waste and disposal management,

**[00:25:45.840] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-000135)  
traffic and transport management, flood mitigation and sewerage management,

**[00:25:51.220] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-000136)  
Pasig River Ferry Service and MMDA Roadside Management Program.

**[00:25:55.360] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-000137)  
These are programs and services,

**[00:25:57.360] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-000138)  
essential to the millions of Filipinos who live, work, and travel throughout Metro Manila.

**[00:26:02.000] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-000139)  
In view of the foregoing, I respectfully urge my esteemed colleagues to support the immediate approval of the proposed budget of the MMDA for Fiscal Year 2027.

**[00:56:41.400] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000638)  
Unquote.

**[00:56:42.340] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000639)

**[00:56:45.780] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000641)

**[00:56:48.240] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000642)

**[00:56:49.700] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000643)

**[00:56:55.060] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000644)

**[00:56:59.680] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000645)  
Unquote.

**[00:57:00.620] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000646)

**[00:57:09.000] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000647)  
Ngayon, bilang sagot po sa issue na yun,

**[00:57:11.620] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000648)

**[00:57:14.720] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000649)  
wala pong paglabag.

**[00:57:15.780] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000650)  
na ginawa ang MMDA as an institution sa data privacy rights ng sinuman.

**[00:57:24.540] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000651)  
Ang sabi niya rito,

**[00:57:27.260] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000652)

**[00:57:39.200] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000653)  
the MMDA categorically denies that it has consented to,

**[00:57:45.780] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000654)

**[00:57:53.700] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000655)

**[00:58:02.900] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000656)  
the MMDA has not legitimized or planned to legitimize

**[00:58:07.520] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000657)

**[00:58:15.800] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000659)

**[00:58:20.300] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000661)  
Earlier, it was mentioned,

**[00:58:22.360] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000662)  
in fact, the videos were uploaded onto the channel of a certain Dada Ku,

**[00:58:27.920] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000663)

**[00:58:31.540] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000664)

**[00:58:35.700] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000665)

**[00:58:38.220] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000666)  
So, parang ang sinasabi po,

**[00:58:41.640] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000667)  
na yung video na yun,

**[00:58:43.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000668)  
kinuha at in-upload sa isang

**[00:58:45.780] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000669)  
private channel called Dada

**[00:58:47.740] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000670)  
Ku. Yes.

**[00:58:49.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000671)  
And therefore,

**[00:58:51.420] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000672)  
walang sabit

**[00:58:53.660] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000673)  
ang MMDA. Tama po ba?

**[00:58:56.600] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-000674)  
Yes, the Honorable

**[00:58:57.660] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-000675)  
Representative Tino, it is a

**[00:58:59.880] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-000676)  
private channel,

**[00:59:01.440] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-000677)  
it is a channel of a private

**[00:59:03.940] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-000678)  
person, and thus

**[00:59:05.700] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-000679)

**[00:59:08.060] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-000680)

**[00:59:10.840] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000681)  
However,

**[00:59:11.480] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000682)  
however,

**[00:59:13.940] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000683)  
kung babalik natin yung

**[00:59:15.440] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000684)  
video kanina,

**[00:59:17.580] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000685)  
yung video na nag-viral po,

**[00:59:20.080] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000686)  
a screenshot of which,

**[00:59:22.440] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000687)  
yan,

**[00:59:23.480] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000688)  
yung video na yan, yan po nag-viral na yan,

**[00:59:27.140] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000689)  
it is on

**[00:59:27.620] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000690)  
the MMDA page,

**[00:59:29.500] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000691)  
on the MMDA Facebook

**[00:59:31.560] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000692)  
page.

**[00:59:36.120] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000693)  
Hanggang ngayon,

**[00:59:37.640] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000694)  
nandun pa rin po.

**[00:59:39.380] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000695)  
Kagabi lang,

**[00:59:40.320] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000696)  
chinek namin, and it is

**[00:59:42.960] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000697)  
on the July 14,

**[00:59:45.440] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000698)  
post, one of the

**[00:59:47.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000699)  
posts on July 14

**[00:59:49.640] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000700)  
of the MMDA page

**[00:59:51.680] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000701)  
itself. So, in other words,

**[00:59:55.780] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000702)  
institutional posting

**[00:59:57.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000703)  
po ito. Hindi po ba?

**[01:00:00.000] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-000704)

**[01:00:11.740] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-000705)

**[01:00:18.260] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-000706)  
That video, that very video, no, it's not a picture.

**[01:00:22.760] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000707)  
It's not a picture. It's the post itself.

**[01:00:27.720] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000708)  
No? Pakicheck nyo na lang po. Facebook account nyo yan.

**[01:00:35.200] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000709)

**[01:00:43.480] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000710)

**[01:00:47.520] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000711)

**[01:00:56.060] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000712)  
Video po yan.

**[01:00:57.420] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000713)  
Yes.

**[01:00:57.720] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000714)  
I-winshot lang namin yun. Ayaw na namin i-play dito yung video.

**[01:01:01.840] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-000715)  
Yes, the Honorable Tino. So the chairman is now double-checking and making sure.

**[01:01:07.420] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-000716)

**[01:01:12.440] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000717)  
Okay. Well, yun na nga po.

**[01:01:14.940] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000718)  
So, at least nalinaw na natin yung policy.

**[01:01:20.100] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000719)  
So may malinaw na policy po talaga.

**[01:01:23.060] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000720)  
Yung mga operatives ng MMDA may document, may use video to document.

**[01:01:27.720] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000721)  
The incidents.

**[01:01:30.520] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000722)  
Pero, no posting to personal logs or accounts.

**[01:01:37.220] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000723)  
Pwede ang institutional posting.

**[01:01:39.600] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000724)  
Okay, that's clear.

**[01:01:41.300] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000725)  
And Mr. Goh is suspended from field operations.

**[01:01:56.720] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000728)

**[01:01:57.420] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000729)  
cases or facing certain administrative cases related to the social media issue.

**[01:02:05.760] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000730)  
So, klaro na po yun.

**[01:02:07.320] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000731)  
Now, ang isang issue ko na lang po ay sa kabila nung policy ng MMDA

**[01:02:13.820] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000732)  
at yung pagkilala nila na dapat i-respeto ang right to privacy ng mga individual.

**[01:02:23.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000733)

**[01:02:25.920] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000734)

No?

So, unfortunately, ang nakita po natin,

**[01:02:40.260] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000739)  
And, as has been pointed out by several institutions,

**[01:02:48.880] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000740)

**[01:02:54.120] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000741)

**[01:02:57.420] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000742)  
eh yung kanyang komunidad, madali siyang makikilala.

**[01:03:02.240] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000743)

**[01:03:09.580] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000744)

**[01:03:16.440] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000745)

**[01:03:24.700] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000746)  
Natutuon namang paglabag.

**[01:03:25.980] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-000747)  
Yes.

**[01:03:26.620] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-000748)  
Yes, the Honorable Madam Speaker.

**[01:03:28.980] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-000749)  
Yes, the Honorable Tino.

**[01:03:30.740] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-000750)

**[01:03:34.160] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-000751)

**[01:03:38.260] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-000752)

MMDA

**[01:07:56.160] Rep. Crispin Diego D. Remulla** (asr-000839)  
Madam Speaker, I move that we open the period of sponsorship and debate.

**[01:08:00.160] Deputy Speaker Mercedes K. Alvarez** (asr-000840)  
Is there any objection? The Chair hears none.

**[01:08:02.160] Deputy Speaker Mercedes K. Alvarez** (asr-000841)  
The motion is approved. Majority Leader.

**[01:08:07.160] Rep. Crispin Diego D. Remulla** (asr-000842)  
Madam Speaker, I move that we recognize the honorable Vice Chairperson Keith Micah,

**[01:08:15.160] Rep. Crispin Diego D. Remulla** (asr-000843)  
Attorney Mike D. L. Tan of the Committee of Appropriations,

**[01:08:20.160] Rep. Crispin Diego D. Remulla** (asr-000844)  
to sponsor the proposed budget of the Department of Justice.

**[01:08:24.160] Deputy Speaker Mercedes K. Alvarez** (asr-000845)  
Is there any objection? The Chair hears none.

**[01:08:27.160] Deputy Speaker Mercedes K. Alvarez** (asr-000846)  
Representative Attorney Mike Tan is hereby recognized to begin the sponsorship of the budget of the Department of Justice and its attached agencies.

**[01:08:38.810] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000847)  
Thank you, Madam Speaker. Madam Speaker, distinguished colleagues, good morning po sa ating lahat.

**[01:08:45.160] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000848)  
It is my honor to sponsor the budget of the DOJ, including its attached agencies,

**[01:08:49.160] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000849)  
for fiscal year 2027, amounting to 46.435 billion pesos, inclusive of the automatic appropriations.

**[01:08:58.160] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000850)  
In terms of new appropriations, which is subject for congressional approval,

**[01:09:04.160] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000851)  
the total budget of the DOJ amounts to 43.305 billion pesos,

**[01:09:10.160] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000852)  
which represents an increase of about 1.13 percent, or 485.028 million pesos higher,

**[01:09:18.160] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000853)  
than the fiscal year 2026 level of 42.820 billion pesos.

**[01:09:26.160] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000854)  
The proposed 46.435 billion pesos budget is broken down by agency as follows.

**[01:09:34.160] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000855)  
Office of the Secretary, 13.930 billion pesos.

**[01:09:38.160] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000856)  
Bureau of Corrections, 11.932 billion pesos.

**[01:09:42.160] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000857)  
Bureau of Immigration, 2.980 billion pesos.

**[01:09:47.160] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000858)  
Land Registration Authority, 3.279 billion pesos.

**[01:09:51.160] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000859)  
NBI, 3.027 billion pesos.

**[01:09:56.160] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000860)  
Office of Alternative Dispute Resolution, 138.756 million pesos.

**[01:10:03.160] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000861)  
Office of the Government Corporate Counsel, 310.168 million pesos.

**[01:10:08.160] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000862)  
Office of the Solicitor General, 2.142 billion pesos.

**[01:10:12.160] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000863)  
Parole and Probation Administration, 1.254 billion pesos.

**[01:10:15.160] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000864)  
Philippine Commission on Good Government, 338.676 million pesos.

**[01:10:25.160] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000865)  
And the Public Attorney's Office, amounting to 7.101 billion pesos.

**[01:10:31.160] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000866)  
This budget will support the department's programs, projects, and its operation

**[01:10:35.160] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000867)  
for the effective, efficient, and equitable administration of justice.

**[01:10:39.160] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000868)  
DOJ, being the government's principal law agency, serves as the government's prosecution arm

**[01:10:45.160] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000869)  
and administers the government's criminal justice system

**[01:10:50.160] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000870)  
by investigating crimes, prosecuting offenders, and overseeing the correctional system.

**[01:10:55.160] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000871)  
It is also the government's legal counsel and representative in litigations and proceedings

**[01:11:00.160] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000872)  
requiring the services of a lawyer.

**[01:11:03.160] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000873)  
It implements the Philippine laws on the admission and stay of aliens within the territory

**[01:11:08.160] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000874)  
and provides free legal services to indigent and other qualified citizens.

**[01:11:15.160] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000875)  
With its many responsibilities, the proposed budget is essential in ensuring

**[01:11:20.160] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000876)  
that the DOJ and its attached agencies have the personnel, facilities, technology, and operational resources

**[01:11:26.160] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000877)  
they need in order to effectively fulfill their respective mandates.

**[01:11:30.160] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000878)  
This will allow the department to strengthen its services, improve the efficiency of case management and prosecution,

**[01:11:37.160] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000879)  
modernize its systems, enhance our correctional and rehabilitation programs,

**[01:11:42.160] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000880)  
continued access to legal assistance,

**[01:11:45.160] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000881)  
and strengthen its capacity to address emerging challenges in law enforcement and border security.

**[01:11:51.160] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000882)  
This will ultimately translate into a justice system that is more accessible, efficient,

**[01:11:56.160] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000883)  
meaningful, and responsive to the Filipino people.

**[01:11:59.160] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000884)  
Thank you, Madam Speaker.

**[01:12:01.160] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000885)  
The representation is now ready to answer questions from our colleagues.

**[01:12:05.080] Deputy Speaker Mercedes K. Alvarez** (asr-000886)  
Majority Leader?

**[01:12:07.160] Rep. Crispin Diego D. Remulla** (asr-000887)  
Madam Speaker, I move that we recognize the Honorable Presley de Jesus

**[01:12:12.160] Rep. Crispin Diego D. Remulla** (asr-000888)  
from PHILRECA Party-list to interpellate

**[01:12:14.160] Rep. Crispin Diego D. Remulla** (asr-000889)  
on the proposed budget of the Department of Justice.

**[01:12:17.160] Deputy Speaker Mercedes K. Alvarez** (asr-000890)  
Representative Presley de Jesus of PHILRECA Party-list

**[01:12:20.160] Deputy Speaker Mercedes K. Alvarez** (asr-000891)  
is hereby recognized for his interpellation.

**[01:12:23.160] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000892)  
Thank you, Madam Speaker.

**[01:12:25.160] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000893)  
May the distinguished sponsor yield some questions from this representation?

**[01:12:31.160] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000894)  
Yes, Madam Speaker.

**[01:12:32.160] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000895)  
Thank you, Madam Speaker, distinguished sponsor.

**[01:12:37.160] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000896)  
This is with regard with the border system modernization and safeguards

**[01:12:42.160] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000897)  
against irregular departures.

**[01:12:45.160] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000898)  
The Bureau of Immigration's project for enhancement of its border management information system

**[01:12:51.160] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000899)  
received approximately 3.8 billion in 2025,

**[01:12:57.160] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000900)  
but recorded only 103.3 million in disbursement, or 2.7 percent.

**[01:13:05.160] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000901)  
Question.

**[01:13:07.160] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000902)  
What is the current implementation status of the border management

**[01:13:12.160] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000903)  
modernization project,

**[01:13:14.160] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000904)  
and which deliverables are already operational?

**[01:13:20.160] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000905)  
Thank you for that question, Madam Speaker, distinguished colleague.

**[01:13:25.160] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000906)  
So, for the E-gate project, there is a delay in the implementation.

**[01:13:32.160] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000907)  
The E-gate project is implemented in three phases.

**[01:13:36.160] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000908)  
The amount

**[01:13:38.160] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000909)  
told by our distinguished colleague is roughly

**[01:13:41.160] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000910)  
accurate, the phase 1 project

**[01:13:44.200] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000911)  
under GAAT 2024

**[01:13:46.380] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000912)  
was already awarded

**[01:13:48.300] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000913)  
only on December

**[01:13:49.820] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000914)  
18, 2025.

**[01:13:51.980] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000915)  
And it's already ongoing

**[01:13:53.820] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000916)  
deployment to Clark

**[01:13:56.060] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000917)  
International Airport, Mactan-Sebu

**[01:13:57.920] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000918)  
International Airport, Davao

**[01:13:59.800] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000919)  
International Airport, Bohol-Pangalaw

**[01:14:02.300] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000920)  
International Airport.

**[01:14:05.800] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000921)  
The implementation

**[01:14:07.160] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000922)  
schedule was affected by circumstances

**[01:14:10.460] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000923)  
beyond the Bureau's

**[01:14:12.340] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000924)  
control. Phase 1, funded

**[01:14:14.000] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000925)  
under the GAAT 2024

**[01:14:16.340] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000926)  
was timely

**[01:14:18.300] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000927)  
subjected to a competitive bidding

**[01:14:20.020] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000928)  
but there was a failure

**[01:14:22.200] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000929)  
of bidding.

**[01:14:25.020] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000930)  
Necessitating a

**[01:14:26.140] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000931)  
re-bidding process but during the re-bidding

**[01:14:28.220] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000932)  
nagkaroon po ng donation ang

**[01:14:30.240] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000933)  
UNIA

**[01:14:30.900] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000934)  
incorporated ng mga

**[01:14:34.380] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000935)  
e-gates. Kaya

**[01:14:36.080] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000936)  
kinailangan pong mag-amend po ulit

**[01:14:38.260] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000937)  
sila ng kanilang bidding

**[01:14:40.000] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000938)  
process.

**[01:14:40.460] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000939)  
Kasi na-cover na po yung ibang

**[01:14:42.100] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000940)  
e-gates na ilalagay.

**[01:14:44.980] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000941)  
So ngayon po,

**[01:14:47.000] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000942)  
yung phase 1 po ay

**[01:14:48.400] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000943)  
ongoing na po. Total of

**[01:14:50.580] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000944)  
47 e-gates po yung

**[01:14:52.520] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000945)  
ilalagay sa iba't

**[01:14:54.700] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000946)  
ibang airport po na nabanggit

**[01:14:56.580] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000947)

**[01:14:58.840] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000948)  
Ang phase 2 po,

**[01:15:00.000] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000949)  
ay already undergoing procurement process.

**[01:15:05.460] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000950)  
Ito po yung underga at 2025.

**[01:15:08.680] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000951)  
So meron pong mga scheduled dates na po

**[01:15:11.040] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000952)  
ng advertisement, pre-bid conference,

**[01:15:13.900] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000953)

**[01:15:15.680] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000954)

**[01:15:18.000] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000955)  
Yung phase 3 na lang po yung gagawin pa po yung

**[01:15:20.600] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000956)  
technical specifications,

**[01:15:23.220] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000957)  
Madam Speaker and Distinguished Colleague.

**[01:15:25.960] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000958)  
Madam Speaker, Distinguished Postal.

**[01:15:27.600] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000959)  
So in short, itong 3.8 billion natin noong 2025

**[01:15:31.360] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000960)

**[01:15:34.200] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000961)  
That's what the Bureau of Immigration is going for.

**[01:15:37.860] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000962)  
Yung phase 1 and phase 2 po,

**[01:15:39.720] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000963)

**[01:15:42.020] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000964)

**[01:15:44.000] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000965)

Yung phase 3 na wala pa pong

**[01:15:57.360] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000971)

**[01:15:58.460] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000972)

**[01:16:01.640] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000974)  
Bureau of Immigration na

**[01:16:03.100] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000975)

**[01:16:04.340] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000976)

**[01:16:07.220] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000977)  
Thank you.

**[01:41:01.900] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001483)  
DOJ responds to the Zamboanga school shooting.

**[01:41:04.540] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001484)  
On August 18, 2026, a grade 9 student opened fire at the high school campus.

**[01:41:11.940] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001485)  
Of Ateneo de Zamboanga University, killing another student and injuring others before taking his own life.

**[01:41:23.360] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001486)  
The incident was live streamed online.

**[01:41:26.740] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001487)  
And the firearms used were reportedly linked to the assailant's father, a Bureau of Customs official.

**[01:41:35.040] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001488)  
The president directed authorities to conduct a full investigation.

**[01:41:38.260] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001489)  
It was the second fatal school shooting reported.

**[01:41:41.940] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001490)  
In the country, within approximately two months.

**[01:41:46.780] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001491)  
Question, which law enforcement agency is presently leading the investigation?

**[01:41:51.960] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001492)  
And what stage will the DOJ National Prosecution Service or National Bureau of Investigation become involved?

**[01:42:00.140] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-001493)  
Actually, ano po, buong DOJ po, yung mga nag-investiga, NBI, meron na pong cases filed

**[01:42:09.400] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-001494)  
against the father of the student.

**[01:42:11.360] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-001495)  
Father of the shooter and the school security guard po.

**[01:42:16.160] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-001496)  
So, they're already filing information before the courts.

**[01:42:24.440] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-001497)  
So, tuloy-tuloy na po yung pag-prosecute po ng ating po nga DOJ dito sa mga kasong ito.

**[01:42:32.380] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001498)  
Yeah.

**[01:42:33.860] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001499)  
Again, Madam Speaker, that will end my interpellation, again, on behalf of my party-list.

**[01:42:41.360] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001500)  
I fully support the budget of DOJ, especially pagdating po sa ating law enforcement agency, yung pong online natin.

**[01:42:53.320] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001501)  
That's why during our plenary debate here, the DICT talaga naman, we condemn yung ating mga online, mga platforms natin.

**[01:43:05.140] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001502)

**[01:43:09.520] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001503)  
Para naman maproteksyon na natin.

**[01:43:11.360] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001504)  
Ang mga kabataan, grade 9 student, napakabata, mag-open fire, and then naka-livestream pa online.

**[01:43:23.080] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001505)  
I think something to do na pagdating ito sa mga social platforms natin.

**[01:43:29.520] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001506)  
Again, thank you very much, Madam Speaker, Distinguished Sponsor.

**[01:43:32.600] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-001507)

for sick

**[01:54:51.380] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001772)

Habang nagaantay pa sila ng

**[01:55:01.280] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001777)

**[01:55:03.280] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001778)  
o humanitarian release, ginoong

**[01:55:05.280] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001779)  
sponsor, Madam Speaker.

**[01:55:56.000] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-001783)  
So, Madam Speaker,

**[01:55:59.440] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-001784)

**[01:56:07.380] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-001785)  
Pero during that time na hinihintay po nila kasi may mga natatagalan pa pong mga pumirma doon sa ating poong EO,

**[01:56:16.620] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-001786)

**[01:56:22.620] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-001787)

**[01:56:28.640] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-001788)

**[01:56:37.880] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-001789)  
Madam Speaker, Distinguished Colleague.

**[01:56:40.120] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001790)

**[01:56:45.440] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001791)

**[01:56:50.140] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001792)

**[01:56:52.620] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001793)

**[01:57:16.480] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-001794)  
Madam Speaker, Distinguished Colleague.

**[01:57:18.160] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-001795)  
As of now, as reported by our Bureau of Correction,

**[01:57:22.620] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-001796)  
Director, isa na lang po yung hinihintay na firma for the EO to take effect dito po yung sa Department of Health

**[01:57:32.740] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-001797)  
kasi kailangan po masertify nila na totoong may sakit yung isang individual, lalo na po yung ating PDL

**[01:57:39.180] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-001798)  
para po makapasok doon sa Executive Climacy na inaayos po ng ating Bureau of Correction.

**[01:57:45.780] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-001799)  
Madam Speaker, Distinguished Colleague.

**[01:57:48.220] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001800)

**[01:57:50.920] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001801)

**[01:57:52.620] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001802)

**[01:57:56.680] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001803)

**[01:58:05.120] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-001804)

**[01:58:11.080] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001805)

**[01:58:17.340] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001806)  
para matulungan ang mga kababayan natin ng mga PDL sa nangangailangan.

**[01:58:22.620] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001807)

**[01:58:31.800] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001808)

**[01:58:34.600] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001809)

**[01:58:41.180] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001810)

**[01:58:45.120] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001811)

**[01:58:51.380] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001812)  
Madam Speaker, Distinguished Sponsor.

**[01:58:52.620] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-001813)

**[01:59:06.780] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-001814)

**[01:59:12.300] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-001815)  
Madam Speaker, Distinguished Colleague.

**[01:59:15.760] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001816)  
Distinguished Sponsor, Madam Speaker, thank you very much for the very candid response of the agency on the matter.

**[01:59:21.920] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001817)  
Pero hindi.

**[01:59:22.620] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001818)  
Hindi po kasi tama na pinaghihintayin natin ng hindi lamang buwan kundi taon na yung ganitong napakahalagang polisiya.

**[01:59:30.780] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001819)

**[01:59:41.960] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001820)

**[02:00:00.740] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-001824)  
Madam Speaker, Distinguished Colleague,

**[02:00:04.560] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-001825)  
ang ginagawa po ng ating Bureau of Corrections,

**[02:00:07.280] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-001826)  
they are coordinating with hospitals that are willing to take in

**[02:00:12.660] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-001827)  
yung ating mga PDL na nangangailangan.

**[02:00:15.800] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-001828)  
For example, meron po silang coordination with East Avenue Medical Center.

**[02:00:22.340] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-001829)  
Doon po nila dinadala, lalaan po sila ng pondo

**[02:00:25.080] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-001830)  
para po madala po yung ating mga PDL na nangangailangan ng medical care

**[02:00:28.560] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-001831)  
sa mga hospital katulad po ng East Avenue Medical Center.

**[02:00:32.940] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001832)  
Maliban po sa East Avenue Medical Center, saan pa po yung iba pang mga facilities?

**[02:00:37.560] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001833)  
Madam Speaker.

**[02:00:47.060] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-001834)  
Lahat po ng facilities naman daw po ay dinadalahan po nila.

**[02:00:52.040] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-001835)  
May mga LGU hospitals naman po silang kamowa,

**[02:00:55.540] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-001836)  
saan rin po nila dinadala.

**[02:00:57.360] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-001837)  
May mga times lang po siguro na dahil puno rin po siguro yung ating hospital,

**[02:01:06.340] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-001838)  
hindi po ma-accommodate, pero we know work out po ng ating Bureau of Corrections

**[02:01:09.980] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-001839)  
na madala po yung ating mga PDL sa tamang pangangalaga rin po

**[02:01:14.340] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-001840)  
ng ating mga medical professionals.

**[02:01:16.840] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-001841)  
Madam Speaker, Distinguished Colleague.

**[02:01:19.120] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001842)  
Madam Speaker, Distinguished Colleague,

**[02:01:21.600] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001843)  
nakatutok ngayon ng publiko sa usapin ng medical releases

**[02:01:24.700] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001844)  
dahil ginagamit ito ng ilang

**[02:01:27.360] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001849)  
high-profile officials na may kasong plunder,

**[02:01:31.140] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001850)

**[02:01:35.280] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001851)  
Ano po ang mga safeguards o stringent monitoring mechanisms

**[02:01:38.900] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001852)  
na pwede ipatupad ng DOJ at Bucor tungkol dito?

**[02:01:42.100] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001853)  
Madam Speaker, Distinguished Sponsor.

**[02:02:17.130] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-001854)  
So, Madam Speaker, Distinguished Colleague,

**[02:02:22.200] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-001855)  
as of now, yung mga kilalang individual po kasi na tangkot or accused,

**[02:02:27.360] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-001856)  
ay nasa pangangalaga po ng BJMP.

**[02:02:31.600] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-001857)  
Pero ang proseso daw po, as per sa ating DOJ,

**[02:02:37.760] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-001858)  
meron pong sariling medical examiner na nagkakondak para po sa BJMP

**[02:02:42.840] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-001859)  
para po masigurado or macertify na ito po yung estado or health status

**[02:02:50.480] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-001860)  
ng ating pong accused na sangkot sa ating pong investigation.

**[02:02:57.360] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-001861)  
And complaints, Madam Speaker, Distinguished Colleague.

**[02:03:01.520] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001862)  
Madam Speaker, Distinguished Colleague,

**[02:03:03.180] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001863)  
hindi po yung mga recently na nakasuhan yung nire-refer ko.

**[02:03:06.180] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001864)  
May mga instances po in the past na nangyari ito.

**[02:03:10.100] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001865)  
But let me just close by saying,

**[02:03:13.100] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001866)  
na usapin ang medical recognizance ay hindi dapat nakabatay sa pangalan,

**[02:03:17.460] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001867)  
posisyon o impluensya ng isang PDL.

**[02:03:20.880] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001868)  
Ang pamantayan ay dapat iisa lang para sa lahat.

**[02:03:24.220] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001869)  
Una, ang aktual na medical condition,

**[02:03:27.360] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001870)  
pangalaba, ang umiiral na batas.

**[02:03:29.960] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001871)  
Pangatlo, ang pangangailangang pangalagaan ang karapatang pantao at due process.

**[02:03:35.680] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001872)  
Kaya hinihiling po natin, Madam Speaker,

**[02:03:38.500] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001873)  
sa Department of Justice, natiyaking ang anumang mekanismo

**[02:03:42.120] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001874)  
para sa medical recognizance, hospital confinement, or medical condition

**[02:03:47.060] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001875)  
ay malinaw, transparent, at pare-parehong pamantayan.

**[02:03:52.360] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001876)  
Dapat walang special treatment, dapat walang VIP lane.

**[02:03:57.360] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001877)  
Maraming salamat, Madam Speaker, Distinguished Sponsor.

**[02:04:00.480] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-001878)  
Thank you, Distinguished Colleague, Madam Speaker.

**[02:04:03.000] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-001879)

**[02:04:08.140] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-001880)  
Thank you po.

**[02:04:08.920] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-001881)  
Salamat po.

**[02:05:41.600] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001898)

**[02:05:48.680] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001899)  
May data po ako dito na for the last 4 years,

**[02:05:55.300] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001900)

Yung nagagagastos ng pamahalaan para sa purchase ng mga e-gates natin,

**[02:06:06.360] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001902)  
including the cameras.

**[02:06:09.900] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001903)  
And the Bureau of Immigration, confirm?

**[02:06:27.120] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-001904)  
So, for the e-gates project po, yung total na kalaan pong pondo,

**[02:06:36.740] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-001905)  
for Phase 1, 1.5 billion.

**[02:06:41.140] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-001906)

**[02:06:42.880] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-001907)  
1.3 po ang Phase 2.

**[02:06:45.620] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-001908)  
And 536 million po ang Phase 3.

**[02:06:52.120] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-001909)  
More or less, mga...

**[02:06:57.120] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-001912)  
More than 2 billion na po yung nagagagastos po para po sa...

**[02:07:02.940] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-001913)  
Ang nagagagastos pa lang po talaga ay 1.528.

**[02:07:09.140] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-001914)  
869.957 billion po.

**[02:07:12.700] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001915)  
There was a...

**[02:07:14.120] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001916)  
How much is the total?

**[02:07:20.200] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-001917)  
Yung Phase 1 pa lang po yung nagagastos po.

**[02:07:24.040] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-001918)  
Madam Speaker,

**[02:07:27.120] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-001919)  
1,528,869,957.

**[02:07:34.120] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-001920)  
1,528,869,957.

**[02:07:39.440] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001921)

**[02:07:46.100] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-001922)

**[02:07:48.040] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-001924)

**[02:07:49.740] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-001925)

**[02:07:52.980] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001926)

**[02:07:54.940] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-001927)

**[02:07:57.120] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-001928)

**[02:08:00.100] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-001929)  
Distributed in Clark International Airport,

**[02:08:05.640] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-001930)  
Mactan Cebu International Airport,

**[02:08:07.700] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-001931)

**[02:08:09.300] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-001932)

**[02:08:13.400] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001933)  
So, 47 na po.

**[02:08:14.640] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-001934)  
47 po yung para sa...

**[02:08:16.660] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001935)  
And the cost of the 47 e-gates is how much?

**[02:08:20.700] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-001936)  
Ito pong...

**[02:08:24.120] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-001937)  
Ito pong 1,500,000,000.

**[02:21:05.810] Rep. Javier Miguel Lopez Benitez** (asr-002213-split-2)  
Madam Speaker, I move that we recognize the Honorable Renee Louise M. Co of Kabataan Party-list to interpellate the proposed budget of the Department of Justice.

**[02:21:14.200] Deputy Speaker Mercedes K. Alvarez** (asr-002213-split-3)  
Representative Renee Louise Co from Kabataan Party-list is hereby recognized for her interpellation.

**[02:21:19.250] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002213-split-4)  
Thank you, Madam Speaker, and good afternoon to the distinguished sponsor and the Department of Justice.

**[02:21:26.040] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002214)  
Will the gentleman yield to a couple of questions in relation to the budget and the policies by which the budget will be anchored on of the Department of Justice?

**[02:21:38.000] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002215)  
Yes, Madam Speaker.

**[02:21:39.440] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002216)  
Thank you, Madam Speaker.

**[02:21:41.160] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002217)  
Madam Speaker, tungkol po ito sa ang unang line of questioning natin, yung nasimula na natin noong budget briefing,

**[02:21:48.700] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002218)  
yung tungkol po sa pag-transfer ng mga persons deprived of liberty ng

**[02:21:56.840] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002219)  
New Bilibid Prison.

**[02:21:58.910] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002220)  
Pwede po bang matanong na ilan ang current number of PDLs sa buong Pilipinas ang nasa Bucor?

**[02:22:07.020] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002221)  
Ilan ang nababawas at nadaragdag ng mga PDLs annually?

**[02:22:26.880] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002222)  
As of July 31, 2026, the PDL population is at 52,356.

**[02:22:45.800] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002223)  
Ang narirelease po kada taon ay it varies.

**[02:22:50.260] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002224)  
So as for 2025 po, ang naririlis po ay...

**[02:22:59.600] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002226)  
10,529. For the first half nitong taong ito, 4,979 po ang na-release. Ang na-admit po for 2025, 9,948. Ang na-admit rin po for 2026, this first half ng taon, ay 4,549.

**[02:23:24.990] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002227)  
So tama po ba? Parang halos equal or magkapantay yung pagdagdag at pagbawas ng mga PDLs sa New Bilibid Prison?

**[02:23:32.860] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002228)  
Halos magkapantay po. Looking at from 2022 to 2026, halos parehas.

**[02:23:39.180] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002229)  
Kung bang makahingi, Madam Speaker, good sponsor ng datos na ito, kung maari ay mag-span po siya ng pano,

**[02:23:48.180] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002230)  
paano, ilan ang nababawas sa dagdagdag sa New Bilibid Prison in the past five years?

**[02:23:54.820] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002231)  
Okay. Yes, Madam Speaker. We will provide the data.

**[02:23:59.620] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002232)  
Maraming salamat po. Yung unang mga tanong po natin kasi sa budget briefing ay,

**[02:24:04.360] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002233)  
yun nga po, paano yung pinaplano na pagkaroon ng pag-transfer ng mga PDLs given na may mga ibang planong gustong gawin?

**[02:24:18.180] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002234)  
Dito sa New Bilibid Prison.

**[02:24:20.180] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002235)  
But following up on my line of inquiry during the committee-level deliberations,

**[02:24:24.980] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002236)  
pwede po bang matanong if the BUCOR has a decongestion strategy or a master plan

**[02:24:30.420] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002237)  
given yung mass transfer of PDLs from the New Bilibid Prison to the penal colonies?

**[02:24:41.160] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002238)  
There is a master plan up to 2028.

**[02:24:46.020] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002239)  
So inaasahan po natin some 53.

**[02:24:48.180] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002240)  
6,000 ay more or less makakompleto na yung pag-transfer by two years from now?

**[02:24:58.100] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002241)  
Yes, because...

**[02:25:00.540] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002242)  
Yes, because magkaklose na nga po yung New Bilibid Prison, Madam Speaker, this is surely...

**[02:25:12.500] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002243)  
Maaaring rin po bang ma-furnish ang representation na ito ng master plan?

**[02:25:18.180] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002244)  
Sa pinagbabatayan, timeline, at kung ano man, para po maisagawa yung mass transfer na ito?

**[02:25:25.560] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002245)  
Yes, Madam Speaker, we will comply po.

**[02:25:29.020] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002246)  
May I ask kung gaano po kadalas na si-check ng Department of Justice or yung BUCOR,

**[02:25:35.780] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002247)  
yung pagsunod doon sa master plan na nakalaan para po sa mass transfer?

**[02:25:48.180] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002248)  
So, they're guided by the master plan.

**[02:26:03.940] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002249)  
Yun po po yung goal po nila from 30,000 sa NBP, nabababa po nila ng 20,000.

**[02:26:11.640] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002250)  
They are looking at...

**[02:26:13.640] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002251)  
Maaaring...

**[02:26:16.300] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002252)  
Yung momentum po for...

**[02:26:18.180] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002253)  
And pursuing the decongestion rate na ideal ay patuloy na hindi implementan naman po ng ating Bureau of Corrections.

**[02:26:29.600] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002254)  
Yung master plan po yung naging guide nila sa pag-transfer,

**[02:26:33.480] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002255)  
kung saan po dadalhin yung ating PDL at yung decongestion rate po ay doon rin po naka-alitsunod.

**[02:26:44.060] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002256)  
Madam Speaker, this is Gish Coli.

**[02:26:47.660] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002257)  
Madam Speaker,

**[02:26:48.180] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002258)  
good sponsor.

**[02:26:49.480] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002259)  
Napansin natin na sa budget ng Department of Justice,

**[02:26:54.980] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002260)  
meron pong nakalaan na 519 million na capital outlay for BUCOR.

**[02:27:01.500] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002261)  
Magkano po dito ay para sa construction or rehabilitation of penal colonies and correctional facilities outside of NCR?

**[02:27:10.420] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002262)  
Meron po ba?

**[02:27:12.940] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002263)  
Or nakatayo na po ba ito?

**[02:27:14.280] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002264)  
So,

**[02:27:14.900] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002265)  
so,

**[02:27:18.180] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002266)  
,

**[02:27:19.180] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002267)  
roughly about 474 million po yung nakalaan para sa pagpapagawa po ng ating Bureau of Correction Facility.

**[02:27:27.800] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002268)  
Nakalaan po ito sa Nueva Ecija po.

**[02:27:31.040] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002269)  
Kasi doon po plano, ilagay po yung ating bagong Bureau of Correction Facility.

**[02:27:38.720] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002270)  
Madam Speaker, this is Gish Coli.

**[02:27:40.760] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002271)  
At tama po na itatayo pa lang ito kung magtuloy-tuloy yung pag-uusap natin.

**[02:27:48.180] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002272)  
At maigrat po ito sa Department of Justice.

**[02:27:51.520] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002273)  
Itatayo pa lang po ito, Madam Speaker.

**[02:27:53.900] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002274)  
Kailan po yung target completion date nitong new penal colony sa Nueva Ecija?

**[02:28:03.040] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002275)  
Hopefully po next year, may tayo na po itong facility na ito, Madam Speaker, at this is Gish Coli.

**[02:28:09.660] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002276)  
Inaasahan din po natin, gaya po ng sinabi sa committee, na maging up to standards ang lahat ng mga facilities at services.

**[02:28:18.180] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002277)  
na itatayo dito sa bagong penal colony sa Nueva Ecija.

**[02:28:25.040] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002278)  
Pero kailangan lang po natin i-check dahil po kung ang target completion, at sana hindi madelay, ay next year.

**[02:28:32.880] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002279)  
Pero may target na matapos yung pag-mass transfer by 2028,

**[02:28:40.360] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002280)  
hindi po sana either maantala o magkaroon ng iba't ibang arrangements.

**[02:28:46.180] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002281)  
Kung saan, habang tinatayo,

**[02:28:48.180] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002282)  
pa yung Nueva Ecija penal colony ay baka ma-transfer sa ibang mga detention facilities ang mga dapat i-a-assign doon sa Nueva Ecija.

**[02:29:01.340] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002283)  
Maybe can I get a guarantee from the Bureau of Corrections na kung ano man po yung pupuntahang mga destinasyon ng mga PDLs,

**[02:29:12.860] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002284)  
gaya po ng sinabi sa committee level meeting, malapit sa pamilya.

**[02:29:18.180] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002285)  
Hindi matatransfer ka sa kung saang penal colony nang walang paalam sa kanilang mga pamilya ng mga lawyers at lahat ng mga tumutulong sa mga kaso nila.

**[02:29:31.100] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002286)  
Madam Speaker, good sponsor.

**[02:29:33.040] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002287)  
Yes, Madam Speaker, the Bureau of Corrections guarantee na as much as possible kung saan po malapit yung pamilya ng PDL, doon po siya maidadala.

**[02:29:44.900] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002288)  
At sana po.

**[02:29:48.180] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002289)  
Ang pagbabago or update tungkol sa kung saan siya, ang isang PDL mapunta, ay ito po ay mapapaalam sa ating pamilya, sa kanilang mga lawyers at hindi mabibigla.

**[02:30:00.000] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002290)  
na nawala na lang po sila doon sa penal colony.

**[02:30:02.840] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002291)  
I assume, and I assume the B Corp is aware of this,

**[02:30:06.240] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002292)

**[02:30:10.100] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002293)  
Yes, we, the Bureau of Corrections guarantee po na ipapaalam rin po ito

**[02:30:15.120] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002294)  
kung sakaling dumating po sa ganong punto.

**[02:30:19.340] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002295)  
Madam Speaker, good sponsor,

**[02:30:20.720] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002296)  
mangingina lang po ang representasyon na ito

**[02:30:23.200] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002297)

**[02:30:28.960] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002298)

**[02:30:34.360] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002299)

**[02:30:37.340] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002300)  
Can that be accomplished by next week, Madam Speaker, good sponsor?

**[02:30:41.460] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002301)  
Yes, Madam Speaker, distinguished colleague.

**[02:30:44.680] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002302)  
Okay, maraming salamat po.

**[02:30:46.740] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002303)

**[02:30:50.360] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002304)  
Ito rin po ay follow-up doon sa tanong natin sa committee-level briefings

**[02:30:54.940] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002305)  
kung saan we confirmed the existence

**[02:30:58.960] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002306)

**[02:31:03.760] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002307)

**[02:31:07.060] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002308)

**[02:31:08.540] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002309)  
Once again, on the floor, Madam Speaker, good sponsor.

**[02:31:28.960] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002313)

**[02:31:55.600] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002314)

na earmarked revenues

meron po tayong

ang understanding ko is

based on the BESF report

7.3.81 billion in non-tax revenues

in 2025 ang nakuleta ng DOJ

hindi po ba pwedeng kunin doon yung

additional na

**[02:52:57.200] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002836)  
hindi po ba pwedeng gamitin yung winning fee?

**[02:53:03.200] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002838)  
as for that amount

**[02:53:05.200] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002839)  
it amounts to 700

**[02:53:07.200] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002840)  
million daw po

**[02:53:09.200] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002841)  
but it is already remitted to the

**[02:53:11.200] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002842)  
treasury

**[02:53:13.200] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002843)  
hindi na po nila magagamit itong

**[02:53:15.200] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002844)  
mga amount na ito

**[02:53:19.200] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002846)  
yan po yung winning fee sa

**[02:53:21.200] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002847)  
civil cases and labor cases

**[02:53:22.030] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002849)  
Sino po ang nakakolekta nun, Distinguished Colleague?

**[02:53:28.180] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002850)  
And why was that? Kung meron pa po tayong kulang in terms of yung lawyer to court ratio, bakit di po natin ginamit?

**[02:53:53.800] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002851)  
So, Madam Speaker, Distinguished Colleague, yung winning fee po na nakukuha po nila sa civil cases and labor cases, through court order, diretsyo na po yun ng Treasury.

**[02:54:10.020] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002853)  
Ang nangyayari na lang po, inihintay po nila na mag-create po ng PAO positions ang DBM bago po magamit yung ama.

**[02:54:22.060] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002854)  
So, it has to go through the DBM.

**[02:54:23.800] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002855)  
Para magamit niyo po yung pera.

**[02:54:28.360] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002856)  
Yes, Madam Speaker, Distinguished Colleague.

**[02:54:30.480] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002857)  
So, what is the process?

**[02:54:33.020] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002858)  
Ano hong nag-request ba tayo sa DBM?

**[02:54:38.020] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002859)  
And when was the request acted upon?

**[02:54:43.920] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002860)  
Or how long does it take for the request to be acted upon?

**[02:54:50.680] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002861)  
And ito pong mga pondo na ito, saan po ba siya nag-request?

**[02:54:53.800] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002862)  
Ano ang nakadeposit?

**[02:54:56.020] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002863)  
Yung winning fee po sa Bureau of Treasury daw po nakadeposit. Diretsyo daw po siya doon.

**[02:55:02.120] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002864)  
So, ito yung earmark?

**[02:55:05.220] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002865)  
Yes, Madam Speaker.

**[02:55:06.700] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002866)  
So, ito yung winning fee, yan po yung...

**[02:55:10.000] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002867)  
Can you explain saan po nanggagaling yung winning fee?

**[02:55:13.620] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002868)  
Kapag favorable judgment po in civil cases and labor cases.

**[02:55:18.700] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002869)  
And this goes to the PAO?

**[02:55:20.640] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002870)  
No.

**[02:55:23.190] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002875)  
It goes to the Treasury po, Madam Speaker, Distinguished Colleague.

**[02:55:27.620] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002876)  
As for the positions po, nakapag-request na po sila ng additional PAO lawyers.

**[02:55:35.300] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002877)  
Ang napaproobahan pa lang po ata for 2027 is 87 positions.

**[02:55:40.120] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002878)  
So 2024 budget, 87, additional 87 pa lang po.

**[02:55:49.800] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002879)  
Ngayon pa lang po mapupunduhan.

**[02:55:51.100] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002880)  
So meron pong gap between the creating and also yung pagpundo po doon sa positions.

**[02:56:00.720] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002881)  
So meron naman pong funding na pumapasok.

**[02:56:03.400] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002882)  
And do we have a target po kung kailan natin ma-achieve yung 1 is to 1 ratio?

**[02:56:10.860] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002883)  
As of now, there's no target.

**[02:56:12.520] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002884)  
That's why they're making a wish list na sana po mabigay yung through Congress yung 500 plus na PAO positions.

**[02:56:21.100] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002885)  
And 400 plus na support staff para po ma-achieve yung 1 is to 1 ratio.

**[02:56:27.280] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002886)  
And also, kapag meron po kasing bagong korte, lagi pong nangangailangan ng dagdag.

**[02:56:33.980] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002887)  
So hindi po po makita kung ano yung final target date kung kailan ma-achieve yung 1 is to 1 ratio hanggat meron pong new trial courts na na-approve or na-create.

**[02:56:48.320] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002888)  
So, Distinguished Colleague.

**[02:56:51.100] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002889)  
Madam Speaker, I think agencies that demonstrate good absorptive capacity like the PAO should be supported with additional funding when there is a demonstrated public need.

**[02:57:01.980] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002890)  
E kitang-kita naman po natin yung pangangailangan.

**[02:57:04.480] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002891)  
Every time nagdadagdag nga tayo ng korte, kailangan pa magdagdag ng PAO.

**[02:57:07.620] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002892)  
So what additional amount can PAO realistically absorb and utilize in 2027?

**[02:57:13.120] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002893)  
Yung binanggit niyo po bang amount, ay kaya niyong i-disperse?

**[02:57:17.980] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002895)  
Yung additional?

**[02:57:19.860] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002896)  
Yung set.

**[02:57:21.100] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002897)  
700 plus billion po, kaya po.

**[02:57:23.640] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002898)  
Kasi purely mga personal services naman po ito.

**[02:57:29.640] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002899)  
So kung tutusin, yung winning fee, pwede niyo pong gamitin sana?

**[02:57:38.500] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002901)  
For additional hirings?

**[02:57:41.660] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002902)  
Pwede pong gamitin siya for additional allowances.

**[02:57:44.360] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002903)  
Pero still, the process is, kailangan pong mag-request ng creation ng...

**[02:57:51.100] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002904)  
PAO positions sa DBM.

**[02:57:53.580] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002905)  
So I urge Congress, our colleagues in Congress,

**[02:57:57.700] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002906)  
tsaka yung ating APRO Chair,

**[02:58:00.560] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002907)  
na sana po, dagdagan po ang pondo ng PAO para magawa po nila yung 1 is to 1 ratio.

**[02:58:05.640] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002908)  
May I go to my next question, Madam Speaker?

**[02:58:11.460] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002909)  
One of the biggest concerns of ordinary Filipinos is how long it takes for cases to be resolved.

**[02:58:17.980] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002910)  
So from the DOJ's end,

**[02:58:19.400] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002911)  
what are the biggest bottlenecks today?

**[02:58:23.320] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002912)  
Lack of prosecutors, support staff, case management systems, witnesses, or coordination with the courts?

**[02:58:31.500] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002913)  
More importantly, what additional budget, personnel, or authority does DOJ need from Congress to help speed up the disposition of cases?

**[02:58:40.380] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002914)  
So there is a need to increase the prosecutorial arm po ng ating DOJ.

**[02:58:46.600] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002915)  
They have already...

**[02:58:49.400] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002916)  
requested sa DBM ng mga additional prosecutorial positions

**[02:58:55.160] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002917)  
para po mapabilis yung ating pong pag-resolve po ng mga kaso.

**[02:59:01.220] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002918)  
As of now, staggered po ang commitment ng ating pong DBM

**[02:59:04.420] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002919)  
na ibigay po yung mga prosecutorial positions.

**[02:59:10.500] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002920)  
And meron rin pong delay sa pag-fill up ng positions na yun

**[02:59:17.080] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002921)  
dahil po dun sa...

**[02:59:19.400] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002922)  
iba pong appointment ay kailangan ng presidential appointment.

**[02:59:24.860] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002923)  
Usually po, umabot ng isang taon.

**[02:59:27.180] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002924)  
Pero alam ko, from the DOJ themselves,

**[02:59:30.580] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002925)  
medyo napapababa na po ng 4 to 5 months, 4 to 6 months,

**[02:59:34.460] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002926)  
yung pag-appoint po ng prosecutors.

**[02:59:38.280] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002927)  
Yung iba naman po, lalo na po yung mga support staff,

**[02:59:41.080] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002928)  
usually sa Secretary of Justice naman na po,

**[02:59:44.740] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002929)  
dumadaan yung pag-appoint.

**[02:59:46.680] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002930)  
Pero malaki po yung pangailangan po para po sa ating...

**[02:59:49.400] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002931)  
ating pong mga dagdag na prosecutors.

**[02:59:52.180] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002932)  
Kasi may ideal number po ang DOJ na at least sana po mapunan

**[02:59:57.200] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002933)  
para po mas mabilis po yung pag-dispense ng ating pong mga kaso.

**[03:00:00.300] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002934)  
Based on the CPBRD report, Distinguished Colleague, DOJ is projected to have 7,140 unfilled positions in 2027, including 1,740 in OSEC. Ano po ba ang target natin ngayon para ma-fill yung gap?

**[03:00:25.060] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002935)  
Actually, yun po yung kulang na ngayon.

**[03:00:26.960] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002936)  
Kasi this is substantial po eh. And this will definitely affect yung disposition of cases.

**[03:00:34.700] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002937)  
Yun po yung hinihintay po yung karamihan po is pending presidential appointment.

**[03:00:41.380] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002938)  
Ilan po ba ang pending due to the presidential appointment? Ilan ang pending dahil sa budgetary concerns?

**[03:00:56.960] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002939)  
As of now po yung hinihintay po na appointment is 203 po na prosecutorial positions.

**[03:01:11.540] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002940)  
As for...

**[03:01:19.540] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002941)  
As for kung ano po yung ine-expect nila na additional positions po para...

**[03:01:26.960] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002942)  
Dumabi po yung ating mga prosecutors, as for 2026, 237 po. Hopefully yung madagdagan.

**[03:01:39.000] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002943)  
2027, 227 and 2028, 237 prosecutorial positions.

**[03:01:45.800] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002944)  
Yung iba pong delay sa pag-fill po ng ating mga positions, yung iba po ay dahil po sa approval naman po ng Civil Service Commission.

**[03:01:55.840] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002945)  
Of the 7,000...

**[03:01:56.960] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002946)  
Of the 7,140 unfilled positions, ilan po ba dun ang prosecutorial?

**[03:02:20.300] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002947)  
866 po yung unfilled prosecutors.

**[03:02:24.660] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002948)  
866 po yung unfilled prosecutors dun sa 7,140 na unfilled.

**[03:02:30.700] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002949)  
And we are expecting 237 will be filled by 2027.

**[03:02:37.100] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002950)  
Additional 227 only.

**[03:02:40.320] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002951)  
So that still leaves us with a substantial amount of around 600 plus.

**[03:02:47.680] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002952)  
And ang problema po dun is mostly budgetary?

**[03:02:50.580] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002953)  
Yes, Madam Speaker.

**[03:02:54.660] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002954)  
Adding to the...

**[03:03:08.460] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002956)  
Yung budgetary constraints po rin po yung main problem po ng ating DOJ.

**[03:03:15.900] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002957)  
Madam Speaker, Distinguished Colleagues.

**[03:03:21.380] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002958)  
The DOJ has approximately...

**[03:03:24.660] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002959)  
P15.3 billion in accumulated earmarked revenues, revenue balances, as of December 31, 2025.

**[03:03:37.080] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002960)  
Pero hindi po ba pwedeng gamitin yung earmarked revenue?

**[03:03:45.160] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002961)  
Para dun sa mga kakulangan natin sa funding?

**[03:03:49.160] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002962)  
Or are these mostly...

**[03:03:52.370] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002963)  
Is this earmarked revenue including attached agencies po ba?

**[03:03:58.100] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002967)  
The DOJ cannot use that.

**[03:04:00.260] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002968)  
There are specific limitations per attached agency as to the allowed spending of that revenue po.

**[03:04:09.820] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002969)  
Lalo na po yung sa LRA, OSG, and...

**[03:04:14.120] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002970)  
How about yung non-tax revenues?

**[03:04:24.500] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-003007)  
Meron tayong P17.381 billion in non-tax collection.

**[03:04:30.640] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-003008)  
Can't we use that?

**[03:04:37.420] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-003009)  
Meron na pong limitasyon yung sa paggamit yung non-tax revenues po.

**[03:04:54.500] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-003010)  
Ano yung... saan po natin siya...

**[03:11:51.500] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-003133)  
On the Bucor, balik po tayo sa Bucor, Distinguished Sponsor, Madam Speaker.

**[03:11:57.400] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-003134)  
Bucor's authorized uniform positions increased from 9,133 in 2025 to 11,133 in 2027.

**[03:12:07.120] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-003135)

**[03:12:09.540] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-003136)

**[03:12:15.300] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-003137)  
May I ask, Distinguished Sponsor, what is preventing Bucor from filling these positions?

**[03:12:21.280] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-003138)

**[03:12:27.700] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-003139)  
Madam Speaker, for the reasons kung bakit hindi pa field itong mga posisyon nito,

**[03:12:35.300] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-003140)  
it is primarily attributable to the additional...

**[03:12:39.540] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-003141)  
Positions created pursuant to DBM-approved restructuring of the Bureau,

**[03:12:44.680] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-003142)  
including 1,000 new positions for correction officers.

**[03:12:48.720] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-003143)  
To facilitate the filling of these positions,

**[03:12:52.360] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-003144)  
the Bureau has requested the approval of a 3-year CSE-approved qualification standards for correction officers.

**[03:13:00.580] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-003145)  
So ito pong qualification standards ay under review pa po ng ating pong CSE office.

**[03:13:06.200] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-003146)  
In the meantime po, yung other vacancies naman,

**[03:13:09.540] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-003147)  
po ay na-address through recruitment and appointment ng processes.

**[03:13:15.360] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-003148)  
Medyo lumaki lang po dahil po dito sa 1,000 new positions na na-restructure po na ating pong DBM.

**[03:13:24.200] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-003149)

**[03:13:27.660] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-003150)

**[03:13:29.320] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-003151)  
In 2025, 33.9% of the 404,124 cases, decided cases, were dismissed by the trial.

**[03:13:39.540] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-003152)

**[03:13:43.420] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-003153)

**[03:13:46.800] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-003154)  
Insufficient evidence, witness problems, weaknesses in investigation, procedural issues, or something else?

**[03:13:54.560] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-003155)

**[03:14:09.540] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-003157)

**[03:14:24.960] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-003158)  
Ang commitment po ng DOJ is that yung standard po na-impose po sa kanila,

**[03:14:34.320] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-003159)  
yung reasonable certainty of conviction na standard,

**[03:14:39.300] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-003160)  
yun po na-impose po sa kanila.

**[03:14:39.520] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-003162)  
At yung reasonable certainty po yung susundan nila para po maiwasan yung pag-dismiss po ng ating mga kaso sa Regional Trial Court.

**[03:14:46.380] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-003163)  
So, it means working closely with the law enforcement agencies na yung available ng evidence,

**[03:14:53.420] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-003164)  
nakahanda na po agad para kung sakali, sa tingin po ng ating prosecutor, medyo solid na po.

yung foundation for filing a case

**[03:15:02.320] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-003166)  
and pursuing a case

**[03:15:03.740] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-003167)

**[03:15:30.360] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-003176)

**[03:55:23.540] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003840)

**[03:55:46.060] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003842)

**[03:56:07.360] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003844)

**[03:57:21.960] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003854)

**[04:15:07.320] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004165)  
So, the General Provisions on Confidential Funds, the General Appropriations Act requires that all agencies using confidential funds must submit a report.

**[04:15:23.780] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004166)  
That agencies utilizing confidential funds shall submit to the President of the Philippines and both Houses of Congress a quarterly report on the accomplishments in the use of said funds.
