**[04:41:15.460] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004470)  
Madam Speaker, Distinguished Colleagues,

**[04:41:19.520] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004471)  
I rise today to present for the consideration of this august chamber,

**[04:41:24.060] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004472)  
the proposed 5 billion budgetary support for the Philippine Reclamation Authority for Fiscal Year 2027.

**[04:41:34.840] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004473)  
Intended for the continued implementation of the Philippine International Exhibition Center, PIEC.

**[04:41:44.940] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004474)  
PRA is the government instrumentality mandated not only to regulate and undertake reclamation,

**[04:41:52.920] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004475)  
but

**[04:41:53.820] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004476)  
also

**[04:41:54.380] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004477)  
to

**[04:41:55.140] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004478)  
pursue

**[04:41:55.540] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004479)  
infrastructure development and the development and management of public estate in the national interest.

**[04:42:05.720] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004480)  
In furtherance of this mandate, the PIEC is envisioned as a national flagship infrastructure project

**[04:42:15.860] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004481)  
that will strengthen the country's capacity to host major international meetings,

**[04:42:23.040] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004482)  
conventions, and exhibitions, support tourism, and related industries,

**[04:42:31.280] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004483)  
and enhance the productive use and value of government-owned properties in the area.

**[04:42:38.980] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004484)  
With this, Madam Speaker, I am ready for the interpellation and in advance,

**[04:42:45.200] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004485)  
I thank our distinguished colleagues for their consideration and support

**[04:42:51.740] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004486)  
for the

**[04:42:53.040] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004487)  
proposed budget of the Philippine Reclamation Authority.

**[04:42:57.240] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004488)  
Maraming salamat po.

**[04:42:58.380] Deputy Speaker Mercedes K. Alvarez** (asr-004489)  
Majority Leader?

**[04:43:00.900] Rep. Ryan S. Recto** (asr-004490)  
Madam Speaker, I move to recognize the Honorable Presley de Jesus to interpellate the sponsor.

**[04:43:06.500] Deputy Speaker Mercedes K. Alvarez** (asr-004491)  
Representative Presley de Jesus of [unclear] is hereby recognized for his interpellation.

**[04:43:11.380] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004492)  
Thank you, Madam Speaker, and to the distinguished sponsor.

**[04:43:17.580] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004493)  
The 5 billion Philippine International Exhibition Center allocation

**[04:43:23.040] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004494)  
provides the Philippine Reclamation Authority with 5 billion pesos

**[04:43:30.460] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004495)  
for the Philippine International Exhibition Center, or PIEC.

**[04:43:35.360] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004496)  
This is a substantial new appropriation for a government-owned or controlled corporation

**[04:43:41.960] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004497)  
that ordinarily undertakes proprietary and revenue-generating activities.

**[04:43:50.160] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004498)  
Question.

**[04:43:51.540] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004499)  
Why is an additional appropriation?

**[04:43:53.040] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004500)  
Why is an additional 5 billion in national government support

**[04:43:55.360] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004501)  
being coursed through the PRA for the PIEC

**[04:43:59.420] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004502)  
considering that the project is being implemented

**[04:44:03.480] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004503)  
by the Department of Public Works and Highways

**[04:44:06.560] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004504)  
for the Office of the President?

**[04:44:12.980] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004505)  
Madam Speaker,

**[04:44:15.500] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004506)  
Your Honor,

**[04:44:17.580] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004508)  
Ang project po natin.

**[04:44:23.040] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004509)  
Ang unang dalawang SARO po kasi ng project ay

**[04:44:30.200] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004510)  
nanggaling na sa Office of the President.

**[04:44:36.520] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004511)  
Pero ngayon po

**[04:44:38.480] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004512)  
humihingi po kami

**[04:44:40.480] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004513)  
siyempre

**[04:44:41.140] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004514)  
ng kaukulang pondo

**[04:44:42.220] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004515)  
dito na sa atin.

**[04:44:44.180] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004516)  
Dahil po

**[04:44:45.280] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004517)  
ang DPWH naman po

**[04:44:50.320] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004518)  
siyempre

**[04:44:51.040] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004519)  
nagkaroon tayo ng

**[04:44:52.480] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004520)  
MOA

**[04:44:55.880] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004521)  
dahil sila ang mas higit na nakakaalam sa implementation ng proyekto.

**[04:45:02.680] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004522)  
Kaya po, nararapat lang na ang DPWH ang mag-implement ng project.

**[04:45:13.120] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004538)  
Madam Speaker, my follow-up question is, what is the precise financial responsibility of the PRA, the Department of Public Works and Highways, and the Office of the President for the project?

**[04:45:25.380] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004539)  
And which entity will own, operate, maintain, and receive revenues from the completed facility?

**[04:45:35.080] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004540)  
Madam Speaker, Your Honor, PRA handles the government asset and project side, while DPWH handles the procurement, design, and construction.

**[04:45:51.840] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004541)  
Ang project naman po ay para sa nasyonal.

**[04:45:55.380] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004542)  
Government under the Office of the President.

**[04:46:02.680] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004543)  
At ito po ay sinama sa list of infrastructure project, flagship project ng pamahalaan.

**[04:46:11.560] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004544)  
Kapag natapos na po ang PIEC, pangangasiwaan po ito ng PRA.

**[04:46:18.360] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004545)  
So, ang revenue po na makukuha dito ay makakadagdag

**[04:46:25.380] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004546)  
sa dibidendo na ibinibigay ng PRA sa pamahalaan.

**[04:46:28.360] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004547)  
So, in short, Madam Speaker, once the project has been completed, PRA na po ang magpapatakbo. Tama ho ba yun?

**[04:46:37.000] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004548)  
Yes po.

**[04:46:39.060] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004549)  
Next, what alternative financing arrangements including PRA corporate funds, land development, revenues, borrowings, or private participation were evaluated before additional national government funding was sought?

**[04:46:53.860] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004550)  
Ito po ba

**[04:46:55.360] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004553)  
Pinag-aralang mabuti bago po kayo humingi ng national government funding?

**[04:47:00.680] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004554)  
Ah, yes po. Ito po ay pinag-aralang mabuti bago po kami humingi ng pondo.

**[04:47:10.320] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004555)  
Ayon po kasi sa CAVCOM approval, NEDA board confirmation at RDC approval, ang national government ang gagastos ng project cost.

**[04:47:24.500] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004556)  
At ang PRA naman, ang contribution niya ay yung lupa.

**[04:47:31.880] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004557)  
Okay. What return, whether through revenue, employment, tourism receipts, or appreciation of public land, should taxpayers expect from this investment?

**[04:47:42.740] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004558)  
Ang financial and economic benefits po ay evaluated ng NEDA.

**[04:47:57.240] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004559)  
Ah, 1.33 trillion pesos, absolute peso value of total net economic benefits over 50 years based on conservative assumption.

**[04:48:12.740] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004560)  
And 377.7 billion pesos, absolute peso value of total net financial benefit over 50 years based on conservative assumptions.

**[04:48:29.900] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004561)  
So, Madam Speaker, if the PIEC eventually generates a profit, will the national government recover its 5 billion contribution before revenues are retained?

**[04:48:42.500] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004562)  
Or distributed elsewhere? Ito po ba ibabalik sa gobyerno o i-dedistribute po sa iba-ibang departamento?

**[04:48:51.740] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004563)  
Yes po.

**[04:48:53.680] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004564)  
Which particular department po or agencies ang makikinabang dito once na-recover po itong 5 billion contribution ng national government?

**[04:49:04.920] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004565)  
Siyempre po, ang makikinabang dito, ang ating unang-una, ang BIR.

**[04:49:12.500] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004566)  
Kasi, siyempre, yung papasok na profit sa atin.

**[04:49:17.180] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004567)  
And ikalawa po, inaasahang lilikha ito ng trabaho, negosyo para sa mga, especially yung sa mga hotels po.

**[04:49:26.820] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004568)  
Kasi napapaikutan siya ng establishment.

**[04:49:31.800] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004569)  
Of course, pag may convention, so yun po ang papasok na income sa gobyerno.

**[04:49:39.780] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004570)  
At ang ikatlo po, lalaki.

**[04:49:42.500] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004571)  
Ang turismo sa Pilipinas, dahil tayo po ay, alam natin, napag-iiwanan na rin natin.

**[04:49:50.600] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004572)  
Napag-iiwanan na tayo kasi wala nga tayong mapagdausan ng malaking mga events sa Pilipinas.

**[04:50:02.340] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004573)  
Thank you, Madam Speaker.

**[04:50:04.320] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004574)  
The PIEC is reported to cost approximately 27 billion.

**[04:50:08.720] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004575)  
Tama ho ba ito?

**[04:50:10.500] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004576)  
27 billion ang total na...

**[04:50:12.120] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004577)  
Yes.

**[04:50:12.500] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004578)  
27 billion.

**[04:50:16.340] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004579)  
And then your target will be this year, 40% lang po ang completion and the rest will be on 2027 by the end of 2027.

**[04:50:28.120] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004580)  
Ito ba yung makukumpleto natin before the end of 2027? Itong project na ito?

**[04:50:33.960] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004581)  
Hindi pa po natin makukumpleto ang proyekto ngayong 2027.

**[04:50:40.020] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004582)  
Ang inish, ang inaasahan po natin makukumpleto.

**[04:50:42.500] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004583)  
Ito ang proyekto ay sa April 2028.

**[04:50:46.120] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004584)  
April?

**[04:50:47.720] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004585)  
2028 po.

**[04:50:48.540] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004586)  
2028.

**[04:50:49.500] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004587)  
Yes po.

**[04:50:53.980] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004588)  
So, my next question, what is the complete funding and implementation plan for the PIEC from initial design through construction, equipment procurement, testing, and opening?

**[04:51:06.020] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004589)  
So, ang kalahatang pondo nga po natin is 27 billion.

**[04:51:16.940] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004590)  
Again, it's related to my first question.

**[04:51:21.140] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004591)  
What portion of the estimated 27 billion cost remains unfunded?

**[04:51:27.380] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004592)  
Magkano po yung kailangan nyo pa po?

**[04:51:29.860] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004593)  
Okay.

**[04:51:33.020] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004594)  
Ang kakulangan pa po natin...

**[04:51:36.020] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004595)  
Pondo ay yung 5 billion at saka yung 10 billion para po makumpleto natin ang ating PIEC.

**[04:51:46.700] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004596)  
So, while phase 1 reached only 90% completion by the end of 2027, kasama doon po ba yung 5 billion na i-appropriate na?

**[04:51:57.860] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004597)  
O ito po'y kukulangin pa?

**[04:52:01.700] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004598)  
Kumpleto po.

**[04:52:02.840] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004599)  
Kasama na yung 5 billion by 2027.

**[04:52:04.820] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004600)  
So, the...

**[04:52:06.020] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004601)  
The 5 billion additional fund, ito na po ang magtatapos o kukumpleto yun?

**[04:52:11.860] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004602)  
No, hindi po.

**[04:52:12.920] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004603)  
Phase 1 pa lang po yun.

**[04:52:14.700] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004604)  
So, yung phase 1 kasama yung 5 billion.

**[04:52:16.920] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004605)  
So, yung phase 2 po...

**[04:52:17.980] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004606)  
Yung phase 2 po, yun yung magtatapos po ng ating construction building, yung PIEC ng 10 billion pa po.

**[04:52:27.800] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004607)  
And that's the remaining?

**[04:52:29.180] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004608)  
Yes.

**[04:52:29.540] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004609)  
Yung sinama nyo na ba?

**[04:52:30.460] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004610)  
Pinaktor na po yung inflation rate, lahat po kasama na dyan.

**[04:52:34.000] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004611)  
Pati yung dollar po natin habang ngayon.

**[04:52:36.020] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004612)  
Tumataas pa?

**[04:52:37.840] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004613)  
Yes po.

**[04:52:38.700] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004614)  
Anticipated na po.

**[04:52:39.900] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004615)  
Okay.

**[04:52:42.560] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004616)  
My next question, climate resilience of a facility built on a reclaimed land.

**[04:52:47.780] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004617)  
Kasi ito po lagi problema natin, no?

**[04:52:49.680] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004618)  
Pag the property is being reclaimed.

**[04:52:52.280] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004619)  
The PIEC is being built on reclaimed land in Manila Bay.

**[04:52:56.140] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004620)  
An area exposed to typhoons, storm surge, sea level rise, land subsidence,

**[04:53:04.140] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004621)  
liquefaction,

**[04:53:06.020] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004622)  
and flooding.

**[04:53:07.180] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004623)  
A facility expected to operate for several decades must be designed for future climate conditions

**[04:53:14.280] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004624)  
rather than only historical averages.

**[04:53:18.400] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004625)  
Question.

**[04:53:19.580] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004626)  
What climate and geotechnical standards were used in designing the PIEC and its reclaimed site?

**[04:53:27.820] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004627)  
Ito po kasi nagiging problema ngayon.

**[04:53:29.700] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004628)  
Pag ano, sa Japan nga po, yung airport nila, every year po,

**[04:53:33.860] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004629)  
nag-ano, nag-a...

**[04:53:36.020] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004630)  
Magsisig, bumababa po.

**[04:53:37.780] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004631)  
Kasi ito po, malaki.

**[04:53:38.980] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004632)  
We're talking about 27 billion plus 10 billion pa.

**[04:53:42.000] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004633)  
Kung sakali po lumabog to or magka-problema.

**[04:53:45.960] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004634)  
Alam niyo po, ang ating bansa, tayo po yung pinakapaborito ng mga...

**[04:53:50.300] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004635)  
ng typhoons.

**[04:53:51.820] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004636)  
Lagi nilang every year, not less than 20 typhoons na dumatid po sa atin.

**[04:53:57.320] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004637)  
Your comment?

**[04:53:58.940] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004638)  
Madam Speaker, Your Honor,

**[04:54:00.520] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004639)  
hindi po ako naniniwala, nalulubog.

**[04:54:05.340] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004640)  
Kasi po, pinag-aralan natin, pinag-aralan ito.

**[04:54:09.120] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004641)  
Hindi lang naman po ang DNR ang nag-aral dito.

**[04:54:12.360] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004642)  
So, una po, sa climate resilient design,

**[04:54:17.920] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004643)  
nagsasagawa po ng risk assessment upang matukoy ang climate related risk.

**[04:54:25.560] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004644)  
Yun na nga po, particular ang flooding, storm,

**[04:54:28.780] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004645)  
ay sinasagawa po at kinakailangan ng resilient measures sa design ng pasis.

**[04:54:35.340] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004646)  
At ikalawa po, para sa flood protection,

**[04:54:39.480] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004647)  
ang structure po ay disinenyong elevate above the applicable base flood levels

**[04:54:46.760] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004648)  
at magkakaroon ng flood barriers upang mabawasan ang exposure sa potential flood.

**[04:54:55.020] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004649)  
At ikatlo po, para sa heat mitigation,

**[04:54:59.120] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004650)  
kasama sa design ang passive cooling strategies

**[04:55:03.180] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004651)  
gaya ng natural ventilation.

**[04:55:05.340] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004652)  
Shading, reflective roofing materials, at urban greening upang mabawasan ng heat gain at energy consumption.

**[04:55:18.840] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004653)  
So, in short, sino ang contractor dito?

**[04:55:20.780] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004654)  
Is it foreign or local po ang nanaro?

**[04:55:24.280] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004655)  
Actually po, hindi pa naman po.

**[04:55:29.240] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004656)  
Yung phase 1 po is EEI.

**[04:55:31.520] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004657)  
So, itong second phase po natin,

**[04:55:34.460] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004658)  
hindi pa naman po,

**[04:55:35.340] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004659)  
nabibid.

**[04:55:37.120] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004660)  
Kung mabibigyan po ng pagkakataon nga

**[04:55:39.080] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004661)  
para matuloy ang proyekto natin na ito,

**[04:55:42.220] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004662)  
ay saka pa lang po ibibid

**[04:55:43.700] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004663)  
pag may pera na itong hawak.

**[04:55:46.740] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004664)  
Ito ang kasi karamihan tinatanong,

**[04:55:49.220] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004665)  
is the project undergone an independent climate risk stress test.

**[04:55:57.620] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004666)  
Meron mo ba kayo na-hire na independent na very familiar

**[04:56:01.440] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004667)  
or ito po talagang kanilang forte

**[04:56:04.420] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004668)  
sa mga ganitong reclaimed site or area or land po

**[04:56:10.260] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004669)  
na climate risk stress test?

**[04:56:13.400] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004670)  
Sino po?

**[04:56:14.000] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004671)  
Meron mo kayong independent na kinuha na mag-survey?

**[04:56:17.580] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004672)  
Meron po.

**[04:56:18.360] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004673)  
Meron po siyempre independent agency na survey po.

**[04:56:22.200] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004674)  
O kung ilan po sila?

**[04:56:23.340] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004675)  
Isa lang ho ba?

**[04:56:23.900] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004676)  
Ano?

**[04:56:44.420] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004680)  
Anong survey po niya na yung Royal Asconis?

**[04:56:49.880] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004681)  
Royal Asconis.

**[04:56:55.840] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004682)  
From, siguro, I guess from Europe to, no?

**[04:56:59.600] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004683)  
Yes po.

**[04:57:00.400] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004684)  
Which particular?

**[04:57:01.880] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004685)  
Dutch.

**[04:57:02.200] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004686)  
Okay, Dutch.

**[04:57:04.420] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004687)  
So, my follow-up question is also related, no?

**[04:57:07.320] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004688)  
Alam nyo, no?

**[04:57:07.800] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004689)  
Once na matapos na natin itong proyektong to,

**[04:57:11.400] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004690)  
hindi lang po dyan nagtatapos yung ating pagbabantay, no?

**[04:57:16.640] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004691)  
Although maganda yung project,

**[04:57:18.760] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004692)  
nagkaroon ng independent stress test,

**[04:57:21.200] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004693)  
pero alam nyo po, ang climate natin, every year, nagbabago.

**[04:57:25.380] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004694)  
Who will pay for the future seawalls,

**[04:57:28.260] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004695)  
ground stabilization or elevation works

**[04:57:31.320] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004696)  
if climate conditions exceed,

**[04:57:34.420] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004697)  
the original assumption?

**[04:57:35.880] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004698)  
Alam po natin, eh,

**[04:57:37.220] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004699)  
pabago-bago po ating panoon.

**[04:57:38.820] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004700)  
So, the good thing that we did today,

**[04:57:41.980] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004701)  
pagdating 10 years from now,

**[04:57:43.360] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004702)  
magbabago na naman yan.

**[04:57:44.600] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004703)  
So, sino po yung magmamonitor,

**[04:57:47.100] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004704)  
yung tinatag nating operating,

**[04:57:49.720] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004705)  
operation and maintenance,

**[04:57:51.060] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004706)  
sino po ang mag-aalaga nito?

**[04:57:54.240] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004707)  
Kairin mo po ba sa PRA

**[04:57:56.480] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004708)  
o meron mo kayong independent na kukunin

**[04:57:59.480] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004709)  
just to maintain itong ating facility?

**[04:58:02.940] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004710)  
We're talking about 27 billion.

**[04:58:04.420] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004711)  
PRA pa rin po, siyempre.

**[04:58:06.180] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004712)  
Yes po.

**[04:58:06.880] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004713)  
Sila pa rin po.

**[04:58:07.820] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004714)  
Yes po.

**[04:58:08.180] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004715)  
So, do you have enough technical people

**[04:58:11.580] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004716)  
to maintain itong seawall natin

**[04:58:15.000] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004717)  
o ano man lang itong kailangan gawin dito?

**[04:58:17.520] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004718)  
Meron po, kasi, di ba,

**[04:58:19.580] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004719)  
nagsimula pa naman po ang reclamation,

**[04:58:21.740] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004720)  
1973.

**[04:58:23.480] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004721)  
Ako po nun ay,

**[04:58:25.100] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004722)  
hindi ako magkakamali,

**[04:58:26.120] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004723)  
apat na taong pa lang ako nun.

**[04:58:28.360] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004724)  
So, nabukin nyo ang edad ko.

**[04:58:29.940] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004725)  
So, wala naman po, di ba,

**[04:58:32.620] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004726)  
wala naman tayong nabalitaan.

**[04:58:34.420] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004727)  
Ganong mga incident.

**[04:58:36.340] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004728)  
That's very enlightening, no?

**[04:58:39.120] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004729)  
For how many years na nireclaim natin,

**[04:58:41.420] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004730)  
not even one,

**[04:58:42.980] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004731)  
na talagang sinabi na tinamaan

**[04:58:46.220] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004732)  
or nagsubside

**[04:58:48.560] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004733)  
or nawala.

**[04:58:49.960] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004734)  
Naging tubig ulit.

**[04:58:51.320] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004735)  
Siguro wala pa nangyayaring ganyan, no?

**[04:58:53.240] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004736)  
In the history of PRA.

**[04:58:55.200] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004737)  
So, congratulations sa inyo, PRA, no?

**[04:58:57.540] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004738)  
For doing a good job.

**[04:58:58.600] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004739)  
Now, I want to go to my second to the last question.

**[04:59:01.860] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004740)  
Status of Manila Bay reclamation

**[04:59:04.420] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004741)  
moratorium.

**[04:59:06.400] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004742)  
The President suspended

**[04:59:07.800] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004743)  
Manila Bay reclamation projects

**[04:59:10.280] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004744)  
in 2023.

**[04:59:12.300] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004745)  
Pending review.

**[04:59:13.900] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004746)  
The suspension was later lifted

**[04:59:15.760] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004747)  
for certain PASAY projects

**[04:59:18.600] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004748)  
after compliance review

**[04:59:20.440] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004749)  
while other projects

**[04:59:21.800] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004750)  
remained suspended pending assessment.

**[04:59:25.320] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004751)  
Question.

**[04:59:26.340] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004752)  
What is the exact status of every

**[04:59:28.580] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004753)  
reclamation project

**[04:59:30.200] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004754)  
covered by the Manila Bay suspension?

**[04:59:32.760] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004755)  
Ilan ho ba yung

**[04:59:33.540] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004756)  
until now?

**[04:59:34.420] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004757)  
Suspended po

**[04:59:37.180] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004758)  
ng mga projects po?

**[04:59:41.960] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004759)  
Your Honor,

**[04:59:43.740] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004760)  
noong August 4,

**[04:59:45.940] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004761)  
2023,

**[04:59:47.820] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004762)  
pinatasan ng Pangulo

**[04:59:49.000] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004763)  
ang DNR at PRA

**[04:59:50.720] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004764)  
na isuspende ang lahat

**[04:59:52.480] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004765)  
ng reclamation projects

**[04:59:54.340] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004766)  
at activities sa Manila Bay.

**[04:59:56.980] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004767)  
Habang nire-review po

**[04:59:58.260] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004768)  
ang compliance ng BAU.

**[05:00:00.000] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004769)  
Bawat proponents sa existing laws, rules, regulation, at environmental requirement.

**[05:00:07.540] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004770)  
Kasabay po ng pagsasagawa ng cumulative impact assessment.

**[05:00:12.480] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004771)  
And sa status po ng Manila Bay Reclamation Moratorium, batay po sa kasalo po yung status or matrix na nasa aming records.

**[05:00:26.320] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004772)  
Sandali lang po at masyadong maliit.

**[05:00:28.120] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004773)  
Tayo po yung...

**[05:00:33.740] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004774)  
Apat po yung nalip na yung suspension. So meron pa pong sampo na ten na hindi pa po nalilip yung suspension.

**[05:00:47.820] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004775)  
So all in all, fourteen.

**[05:00:49.680] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004776)  
Yes po.

**[05:00:50.320] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004777)  
Fourteen. How many were granted?

**[05:00:54.200] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004778)  
Na nagtuloy pa rin. Remember there was a suspension in the...

**[05:00:57.960] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004779)  
Under review. And then tunuloy po. Diba?

**[05:01:00.800] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004780)  
So ilan po yung ongoing projects ngayon?

**[05:01:03.320] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004781)  
Apat po.

**[05:01:04.140] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004782)  
Apat?

**[05:01:04.840] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004783)  
Yes po.

**[05:01:05.500] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004784)  
So hindi pala fourteen. Four at ten yung suspension.

**[05:01:09.540] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004785)  
Four at ten po.

**[05:01:10.540] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004786)  
Okay. So in totality, ilan po ang total hectares natin sa Manila Bay ngayon?

**[05:01:16.540] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004787)  
Yung ginagawa at saka yung dati na nagawa?

**[05:01:38.980] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004788)  
Your Honor, ang ating pong ongoing projects ay 625 hectares.

**[05:01:50.340] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004789)  
At yung mga dati po na nakalagay ngayon sa Manila Bay, yung MOA, yung mga hotels doon, ilan po yun total?

**[05:01:57.960] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004790)  
700 hectares po, Your Honor.

**[05:02:02.800] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004791)  
So all in all, sa Manila Bay, reclaimed lang, nasa almost 1,325 hectares?

**[05:02:10.420] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004792)  
Yes po, Your Honor.

**[05:02:11.420] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004793)  
So, ito, hypothetical po, magmula po doon sa Ross Boulevard,

**[05:02:19.240] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004794)  
kung saan doon talaga ano, going there to 1,600, 1,325,

**[05:02:25.000] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004795)  
mga ilang kilometro po ang ating na-reclaimed?

**[05:02:27.960] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004796)  
All the way po, hanggang doon.

**[05:02:30.440] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004797)  
So this is a ballpark figure.

**[05:02:32.340] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004798)  
But at least may idea tayo kung gano'n nakahaba yung ating na-re-reclaimed.

**[05:02:37.460] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004799)  
Your Honor, more or less po, seven kilometers.

**[05:02:41.980] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004800)  
From the start ng Ross Boulevard pa gano'n, seven kilometers?

**[05:02:46.000] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004801)  
Yes po.

**[05:02:47.300] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004802)  
Hindi ko na itatanong kung gano'n nakalalim yung mga tubig doon.

**[05:02:50.600] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004803)  
Sigurado ko, it's going to be deep.

**[05:02:52.620] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004804)  
So, I'm going to be, my last question, kasi maraming magtatanong din tungkol dito.

**[05:02:57.960] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004805)  
So, I would like to end my question na cumulative environment impact on Manila Bay.

**[05:03:04.020] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004806)  
An environmental compliance certificate typically evaluates an individual project.

**[05:03:11.320] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004807)  
Manila Bay, however, may be affected by the combined effects of multiple reclamation projects

**[05:03:17.780] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004808)  
on water circulation, flooding, fisheries, sediment, movement, and coastal ecosystem.

**[05:03:26.280] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004809)  
Question.

**[05:03:27.480] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004810)  
What?

**[05:03:27.940] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004811)  
What is the present status of the government's cumulative impact assessment for Manila Bay reclamation?

**[05:03:35.240] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004812)  
What has been completed to date?

**[05:03:37.860] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004813)  
And when will the final assessment be available?

**[05:03:54.880] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004814)  
Your Honor, yung phase one po natin is,

**[05:03:57.940] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004815)  
completed na po yun.

**[05:04:00.660] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004816)  
So, ang status po ay ongoing po yung iba kasi sinasagawa ng DNR.

**[05:04:13.060] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004817)  
May mga regulatory options ang government.

**[05:04:18.040] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004818)  
And ito po ay may mga additional mitigation, redesign, or modification imposed strictly condition.

**[05:04:26.240] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004819)  
So, Your Honor,

**[05:04:27.940] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004820)  
as I said, completed na po ang phase one ng cumulative impact assessment or CIA na ay sinasagawa ng DNR

**[05:04:37.820] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004821)  
sa pamamagitan ng Marine Environmental and Resources Foundation Incorporated.

**[05:04:45.180] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004822)  
Ang resulta po ng phase one CIA study para sa Manila Bay reclamation project

**[05:04:50.700] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004823)  
ay tinalakay na rin po ng DNR noong December 2025.

**[05:04:55.680] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004824)

**[05:05:07.380] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004825)  
upang maisagawa ang findings at recommendation ng CIA sa Environmental Compliance Certificate or ECC Condition at Environmental Impact.

**[05:05:21.260] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004826)  
Your Honor.

**[05:05:22.880] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004827)

**[05:05:25.680] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004828)

**[05:05:35.200] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004829)

**[05:05:46.120] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004830)

**[05:06:24.960] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-004837)

na mauulit yung ganong mga

**[05:14:55.400] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-005036)  
pangyayari.

**[05:14:57.280] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-005037)  
Marami pong salamat Madam Speaker, Distinguished Sponsor.

**[05:15:00.220] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-005038)  
Balikan ko lang po yung nabanggit kanina na pronouncement ng Pangulong Marcos noong 2023

**[05:15:05.940] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-005039)  
regarding the suspension of reclamation projects in the Manila Bay.

**[05:15:11.180] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-005040)  
Earlier, binanggit po ninyo, the suspension has been lifted. For the record po, tama po ba?

**[05:15:17.120] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-005041)  
Yes po. Apat po yung na-lifted. Meron pa po tayong sampung suspended.

**[05:15:24.620] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-005042)  
So, apat po ang lifted. Mayroon pang sampo na suspended pa.

**[05:15:31.320] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-005043)  
So, ano po ang mangyayari dito po sa sampung projects na suspended pa?

**[05:15:36.600] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-005044)  
Hanggat hindi po sila nakakapagpasa ng requirements na nire-require ng DNR,

**[05:15:43.540] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-005045)  
hindi po sila mabibigyan, hindi po malilip yung kanilang suspension.

**[05:15:49.840] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-005046)  
Madam Speaker, Distinguished Sponsor, maliban po sa nabanggit ninyo na isang requirement,

**[05:15:54.880] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-005047)  
para matanggal ang kanilang suspension o ma-lift ang suspension nila ay yung submission

**[05:15:59.900] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-005048)  
ng requirements mula sa DNR. Ano pa po yung dahilan? Bakit suspended ang sampung projects na ito?

**[05:16:11.120] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-005049)  
Yung result po kasi ng cumulative impact ay hindi pa po lumalabas.

**[05:16:17.200] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-005050)  
Kaya po, hindi pa nalilip yung kanilang suspension, Your Honor.

**[05:16:22.320] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-005051)  
Doon naman po. Maraming salamat, Madam Sponsor.

**[05:16:24.620] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-005052)  
Madam Speaker, doon naman po sa apat na na-lift yung suspension, maaari po bang pangalanan natin?

**[05:16:31.520] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-005053)  
Ano-ano itong apat na proyektong ito? At sino po ang nag-apply ng permiso?

**[05:16:46.960] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-005054)  
Your Honor, ang lifted na po ang suspension ay ang Manila Waterfront City.

**[05:16:54.620] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-005055)  
Pasay SM Reclamation Project, Pasay Harbor City, at ang Bacoor City na 90 hectares inner island.

**[05:17:08.440] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-005056)  
Pwede rin po bang pangalanan, Madam Speaker, Distinguished Sponsor, kung sino ang proponent?

**[05:17:12.760] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-005057)  
Yung mga kumpanya behind. Kasi po, alam ko, conglomerate yung ilan dito sa apat na proyektong ito.

**[05:17:54.620] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-005067)  
Your Honor, ang may-ari po ng Manila Waterfront City ay ang Manila Waterfront Corporation.

**[05:18:14.640] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-005068)  
Ito naman pong PASAIS SM ay SM Corporation po.

**[05:18:21.440] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-005069)  
And ang PASAIS Harbor City po, ang nagmamay-ari po ay ang PASAIS City Local Government, PASAIS Harbor City Corp.

**[05:18:37.060] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-005070)  
And yung pong Bacoor City na 90 hectares ay pag-aari po ng Probel and Robinson, Your Honor.

**[05:18:46.800] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-005071)  
Yung pong sa huli, yung sa Bacoor po, sino po muli?

**[05:18:51.440] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-005072)  
Probel and Robinson.

**[05:18:54.000] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-005073)  
Maraming salamat. Thank you, Madam Sponsor, Madam Speaker.

**[05:18:59.860] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-005074)  
Doon naman po sa mga suspended na mga permits, ano po ang ginagawa ng PRA dito sa sampu na ito?

**[05:19:09.780] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-005075)  
Meron po ba tayong follow-through o hinaya-alaman po natin silang makakompleto ng kanilang mga requirements?

**[05:19:16.740] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-005076)  
At ano po yung timeline natin para sa sampu ito?

**[05:19:21.440] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-005077)  
About po sa mga suspended natin, depende po yun kung kailan nila matatapos yung ma-comply yung mga requirements na kailangan ma-submit po nila.

**[05:19:43.120] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-005078)  
So, wala po talaga siyang timeline.

**[05:19:47.560] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-005079)  
Depende po yun kung mapapaaga nila na makapag-aaral.

**[05:19:51.440] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-005080)  
Makakomply sila. Nasa sa kanila po yun, Your Honor.

**[05:19:55.500] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-005081)  
Hindi po ba napapaso, Madam Speaker, Madam Sponsor, yung mga ganitong application kung sakali pong hindi nakakakomply fully yung applicant?

**[05:20:06.440] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-005082)  
Your Honor, depende po yun sa ECC kung papayagan nilang ma-extend.

**[05:20:13.560] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-005083)  
So, halimbawa po itong sampu na ito, hanggang kailan po natin naasahan na po pwede silang makakomply doon sa requirement?

**[05:20:21.440] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-005084)  
Ang live po ng ECC ay five years. So, after five years po at hindi pa sila nakapag-comply, pwede po silang i-apply for extension, Your Honor.

**[05:20:42.440] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-005085)  
Maraming salamat, Madam Speaker, Madam Sponsor.

**[05:20:45.560] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-005086)  
Napakahalaga po kasi itong suspension na binanggit originally ng Presidente, which was eventually lifted.

**[05:20:51.440] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-005087)  
Kasi meron ding apat na malalaking kumpanya na natanggalan din ng suspension at nag-proceed yung kanilang mga proyekto.

**[05:20:59.240] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-005088)  
At sa panahon po ngayon na tumitingin talaga ang pagbaha sa Metro Manila at karating pong mga probinsya natin,

**[05:21:05.600] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-005089)  
talaga pong isa sa tinitingnan ng ating mga kababayan na maaaring dahilan kung bakit ang baha ay hindi ganun kabilis mawala tulad dati na pagbumaha after a few hours

**[05:21:15.540] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-005090)  
or matagal na yung a few days ay bababa dahil po sa nangyayari yung mga developments na ito.

**[05:21:21.440] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-005091)  
Ang tanong po natin ay, how can dredging and reclamation affect the worsening of flooding episodes in the country, particularly in NCR and our nearby provinces?

**[05:21:51.440] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-005092)  
Kaya nga po, nire-require namin na subject po ay for hydrodynamic modeling, flooding studies, and hydraulic studies.

**[05:22:06.120] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-005093)  
So ito pong studies na ito, sino po ang nagko-commission?

**[05:22:13.240] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-005094)  
UP po, Your Honor.

**[05:22:15.520] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-005095)  
Sino po ang nagko-commission sa UP?

**[05:22:20.320] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-005096)  
Yung proponent po.

**[05:22:21.440] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-005097)  
Your Honor.

**[05:22:23.460] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-005098)  
Sige po, mukhang mahalaga po na sa sitwasyon po natin ngayon, hindi dapat sapat na mayroon lamang study na kinomission yung proponent.

**[05:22:31.680] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-005099)  
The PRA should exercise due diligence in ensuring na ito po ay hindi makaka-apekto sa pagpapalala lalo ng baha sa Metro Manila at mga karating na po.

**[05:22:42.640] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-005100)  
Yung sumunod ko naman po ang tanong, Madam Sponsor, Madam Speaker, ay paano po ang coastal defense ng mga reclaimed areas?

**[05:22:51.440] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-005101)  
Kasi po sila na ngayon yung bago na nasa coast natin. So ano po ang coastal defense mechanisms na mayroon, kung mayroon man, itong mga proyektong ito?

**[05:23:04.280] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-005102)  
Kasi baka po sa dulo, sila naman yung susunod na maging problema natin pagdating ng mga storm surge mula po sa area na yan.

**[05:23:25.000] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-005111)  
Ah dati po kasi ang seawall natin ay 3 meters lang. So ngayon po, ginawa na po natin siyang 7 meters.

**[05:23:32.600] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-005112)  
So sino po ang magkoconstruct ng seawall dito po sa mga reclaimed areas na ito? Sino po ang gagastos?

**[05:23:41.900] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-005113)  
Yung proponent na po, kasi po part po ito ng project.

**[05:23:45.600] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-005114)  
Kumusta po yung compliance nila dito po sa provision na ito ng kontrata?

**[05:23:49.340] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-005115)  
Um, ongoing po, Your Honor.

**[05:23:56.160] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-005116)  
And well, monitored po yun, Your Honor.

**[05:24:00.600] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-005117)  
Madam Speaker, Madam Sponsor, may request the PRA to submit a report on the compliance on the coastal defense measures that are installed or are being implemented by the proponents of the project.

**[05:24:14.420] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-005118)  
Yes, Your Honor.

**[05:24:15.440] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-005119)  
Thank you very much, Madam Sponsor, Madam Speaker.

**[05:24:17.860] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-005120)  
I cannot overemphasize yung fear ng mga kababayan natin sa lumalalang pagbaha na naranasan natin ngayon.

**[05:24:24.200] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-005121)  
Maaring patikim pa lang ito nangyayari sa atin at alam natin na isa sa tinitindahan ng ating mga kababayan ay mga pagkarami-raming reclamation projects na nangyayari sa Manila Bay.

**[05:24:34.900] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-005122)  
Kailangan natin suriin ang kaugnayan ng mga reclamation projects sa lumalalang pagbaha sa Metro Manila at mga karating na probinsya.

**[05:24:42.400] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-005123)  
We need a comprehensive approach to flood mitigation and management.

**[05:24:45.720] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-005124)  
Hindi dapat inaaprobahan ng maramigyan.

**[05:24:47.860] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-005125)  
Sa mga reclamation projects na walang maayos na impact assessment sa flooding sa mga komunidad at walang proper drainage.

**[05:24:54.520] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-005126)  
Nakababahala na ang total approved reclamation projects sa Manila Bay, 13 in total ay around 5,000 hectares or about 8% of the total land area of the National Capital Region.

**[05:25:05.200] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-005127)  
Pero hanggang sa ngayon, wala tayong nakikita maayos na integration program para ting sa flood management between these projects and the LGUs around the Manila Bay.

**[05:25:17.860] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-005128)  
Madam Speaker, gusto po natin magpasalamat sa ating butihing sponsor at sana po ay makapag-comply ang PRA doon po sa nire-request natin ng mga dokumento.

**[05:25:27.520] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-005129)  
Marami pong salamat.

**[05:25:28.780] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-005130)  
Marami salamat po, Your Honor.

**[05:25:31.280] Deputy Speaker Mercedes K. Alvarez** (asr-005131)  
Majority Leader.

**[05:25:37.940] Rep. Ryan S. Recto** (asr-005132)  
Madam Speaker, I move to recognize the Honorable Sarah Jane Elago to interpolate the sponsor.

**[05:25:42.940] Deputy Speaker Mercedes K. Alvarez** (asr-005133)  
Representative Sarah Jane Elago of Gabriela Women's Party List is hereby recognized.

**[05:25:47.860] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-005134)  
Maraming salamat, Madam Speaker. Sundan ko lang po yung nabuksan na usapin ng ating kasamahan tungkol sa reclamation sa Manila Bay.

**[05:26:01.940] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-005135)  
Ito po ay tungkol doon sa na-aprobahan na na dalawang reclamation projects sa Pasay City.

**[05:26:08.240] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-005136)  
Sang-ayon sa statement ng PRA noong November 24, 2023, pagkatapos ng rigorous evaluation,

**[05:26:17.860] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-005137)  
nakasama ang DENR, ang mga proyektong ito ay nag-adhere na sa mga kondisyon na sinet para sa Environmental Compliance Certificate at Area Clearance.

**[05:26:27.080] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-005138)  
Ang dalawang proyektong ito ay ang 265-hectare Pasay Harbor City Reclamation Project ng Pasay Harbor City Corporation

**[05:26:35.300] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-005139)  
at ang 360-hectare Pasay Reclamation Project ng SM Prime Holdings.

**[05:26:43.260] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-005140)  
Buong 5 billion peso budget ng PRA para sa 2027 ay nakalaan.

**[05:26:47.860] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-005141)  
Para sa MOOE ng Philippine International Exhibition Center na matatagpuan sa 265-hectare na Pasay Harbor City.

**[05:26:56.120] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-005142)  
Ang katanungan ko po, Madam Speaker, sa kabila po ng naisagawang cumulative impact assessment sa Manila Bay reclamation

**[05:27:04.160] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-005143)  
na mayroong matinding epekto sa kabuhayan ng mga manging isda, water circulation, traffic management at pagbaha,

**[05:27:13.260] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-005144)  
bakit po ba ipinagpapatuloy pa rin ng PRA ang mga proyektong ito?

**[05:27:24.860] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-005146)  
Your Honor, yung mga nabanggit po na pinayagan magpatuloy matapos ang compliance review ay ang 265-hectare Pasay City Harbor and Development Project

**[05:27:41.720] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-005147)  
ay pinayagang mag-resume noong 2023 matapos ang DNR compliance review.

**[05:27:47.860] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-005148)  
Na nag-kumpirma ng kanilang compliance sa kanilang Environmental Compliance Certificates or ECCs, area clearance at mga requirements ng PRA.

**[05:28:00.300] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-005149)  
Gayunman, ang kanilang pagpapatuloy ay mananatiling subject sa continuing monitoring at sa risulta ng cumulative impact assessment ng DNR.

**[05:28:12.740] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-005150)  
And mahalaga pong banggitin na sa Pasay 360, binago mismo ang configuration ng

**[05:28:17.860] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-005151)  
reclamation upang magkaroon ng mas malawak na water channel sa pagitan ng mainland at reclaim island. Ang unang redesign po ay naglaan ng 300-meter channel at pagkatapos ay pinalawak

**[05:28:37.980] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-005152)  
patungo humigit-kumulang 476 meters bilang bahagi ng mga hakbang upang tugunan ang hydrodynamic at flooding

**[05:28:47.860] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-005153)  
concerns. Sa katunayan po, may bahagi ng dating reclaim na kinakailangang alisin o i-rehandle upang makagawa ng mas malawak na channel.

**[05:29:01.240] Rep. Josefina B. Tallado** (asr-005154)  
Ito nga po ay yung sa area sa tapat ng dating Sofitel Hotel. Your Honor.

**[05:29:10.420] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-005155)  
Maraming salamat Madam Speaker. Nabanggit nga ako na kung sa papel naman pagdating sa mga requirements,

**[05:29:15.840] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-005156)  
na punan ito, na isumiti ito, na aprobahan. Ngunit ang nakita rin po natin sa deliberasyon dito sa plenaryo, halimbawa,

**[05:29:24.520] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-005157)  
at dun sa mga naging budget briefings sa lahat po ng mga issues na may kinalaman sa Manila Bay Clean Up, sa Manila Bay Reclamation,

**[05:29:33.840] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-005158)  
at sa panawagan natin ng Manila Bay Rehabilitation, ay wala pong particular na programa na sapat para matugunan yung impact

**[05:29:44.500] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-005159)  
sa ating mga mangingisipan.

**[05:29:45.840] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-005160)  
Sa lahat po ng mga nakasalalay, ang kanilang kabuhayan, hindi lang po sa particular area na ito, pero dun sa buong Manila Bay mismo, marami na pong mga pag-aaral ang tungkol sa

**[05:30:00.000] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-005161)  
Matindi na epekto nito sa ating mga fisherfolk, asang-ayon po sa pamalakaya na wala na po ang kanilang over 80% ng kanilang daily income dahil daw po sa mga reclamation projects na nasira na ang mga major fishing grounds, ang fish habitats, kabilang na ang mangroves, coral areas at seagrasses.

**[05:53:54.600] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-005525-split-2)  
Madam Speaker, distinguished members of the House of Representatives, ladies and gentlemen, I rise to sponsor the proposed budget of the Department of Migrant Workers, including its attached agency the Overseas Workers Welfare Administration or OWWA, for fiscal year 2027,

**[05:54:18.940] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-005526)  
amounting to an expenditure of 10.68 billion. Since its establishment in 2022, the DMW has stayed true to its mandate to protect the rights, preserve the dignity, and promote the welfare of more than 2.2 million overseas Filipino workers across the globe. They serve as a tahanan for our OFWs, whose remittances reached

**[05:54:44.380] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-005527)  
a record of US$35.63 billion in 2025, accounting for 8% of our national GDP. The DMW commitment to our OFWs manifests through its key programs and welfare initiatives, such as legal, medical, and other assistance through the Aksyon Fund, repatriation assistance through the OWWA Emergency Repatriation Fund,

**[05:55:14.380] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-005528)  
specialized medical care through the OFW Hospital, national reintegration services, anti-illegal recruitment and anti-trafficking operations, and maritime research and skills services, among many others. Knowing the significant economic contributions of OFWs and this government's obligation to protect their rights and welfare along the global volatility and evolving geopolitical

**[05:55:44.380] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-005529)  
realities they are facing, it is imperative for this Congress to equip the DMW with the capacity to deliver its call to provide agile and responsive service befitting the hard work and sacrifice of each OFW and their families. And so, Madam Speaker, we solicit your kind support for the swift approval of the budget of the DMW and attached agency for fiscal year 2027.

**[05:56:14.380] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-005530)  
We are now ready to address any questions and hope for a meaningful exchange with our distinguished colleagues. Majority Leader.

**[05:58:44.380] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-005535)

**[06:25:57.200] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006138)

I would cater 1,000 new scholars

**[06:27:05.200] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006162)

**[06:27:07.380] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006163)

ah,

60,000 pesos
