**[06:29:42.760] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006294)  
the additional 1 billion for the Action Fund

**[06:29:44.960] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006295)  
will cover,

**[06:29:46.280] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006296)  
how many OFWs will be assisted

**[06:29:48.280] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006297)  
under each type of assistance,

**[06:29:50.000] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006298)  
and what is the target for the action fund.

**[06:29:51.360] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006299)  
And what is the target processing and release time?

**[06:30:00.000] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006300)  
Madam Speaker, Distinguished Colleague,

**[06:30:16.600] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006301)  
the additional 1 billion pesos po for the Action Fund

**[06:30:20.200] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006302)  
would benefit about 20,000 OFWs at the minimum

**[06:30:24.500] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006303)  
from various services, legal, financial, or medical.

**[06:30:30.000] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006304)  
At ang target po ng disbursement ng mga assistance po

**[06:30:37.140] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006305)  
ay 30 days, although depende rin po yan

**[06:30:40.120] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006306)  
sa availability ng funds.

**[06:30:42.620] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006307)  
But the department is committed to delivering

**[06:30:48.040] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006308)  
the disbursement within 30 days po.

**[06:30:50.800] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006309)  
Ano-ano po ang klase ang assistance?

**[06:30:53.800] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006310)  
Legal, financial, or medical po.

**[06:30:56.460] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006311)  
And ang target processing time from application

**[06:30:59.720] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006312)  
is 30 days.

**[06:31:00.780] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006313)  
30 days po ang target.

**[06:31:03.780] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006314)  
Okay, Madam Speaker, through you,

**[06:31:07.180] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006315)  
may I also ask our Distinguished Colleague

**[06:31:09.360] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006316)  
to state, ano po ba ang corrective measures

**[06:31:13.560] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006317)  
that have already been implemented,

**[06:31:15.940] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006318)  
not merely proposed,

**[06:31:17.900] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006319)  
to address COAS findings on doubtful claims,

**[06:31:22.020] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006320)  
ineligible beneficiaries, incomplete documents,

**[06:31:24.980] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006321)  
and the absence of uniform classifications for illnesses.

**[06:31:29.720] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006322)  
Injuries and mental health cases.

**[06:31:34.780] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006323)  
Madam Speaker, Distinguished Colleague,

**[06:31:37.100] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006324)  
Action Fund Information System is a COA recommendation

**[06:31:41.320] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006325)  
which is now being implemented.

**[06:31:44.000] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006326)  
And magdadagdag rin po ng mga amendments

**[06:31:46.540] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006327)  
to the existing guidelines,

**[06:31:49.920] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006328)  
as well as in compliance with the COA recommendations.

**[06:31:53.340] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006329)  
Yun po ang ginagawa po ng BMW ngayon.

**[06:31:57.180] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006330)  
Thank you.

**[06:31:59.720] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006331)  
Do you already have a system in place?

**[06:32:01.420] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006332)  
Yes, it's currently implemented.

**[06:32:02.280] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006333)  
And this is what?

**[06:32:03.580] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006334)  
This is digital?

**[06:32:05.380] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006335)  
Digitalized na po.

**[06:32:07.240] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006336)  
Are we using AI?

**[06:32:22.940] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006337)  
Yes, it's a digitalized system po.

**[06:32:26.080] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006338)  
And AI would be a good tool po.

**[06:32:29.720] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006339)  
To add safeguards and help with the verification po

**[06:32:34.980] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006340)  
of these double claims.

**[06:32:36.580] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006341)  
So mayroon na po tayong clearance from COA?

**[06:32:39.000] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006342)  
Or we are already addressing that?

**[06:32:42.660] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006343)  
Yes, it's implemented na po.

**[06:32:44.780] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006344)  
Okay.

**[06:32:45.580] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006345)  
Thank you for that, Distinguished Sponsor.

**[06:32:48.160] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006346)  
May I ask, the DMW also is requesting

**[06:32:51.000] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006347)  
60 million for OFW Servicio Ban, Caravan,

**[06:32:54.880] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006348)  
60 million for International Labor Affairs,

**[06:32:58.220] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006349)  
180 million for...

**[06:32:59.720] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006350)  
G, General Administration and Support Services,

**[06:33:03.380] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006351)  
and 700 million for Key Services.

**[06:33:06.380] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006352)  
For each component,

**[06:33:08.380] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006353)  
what exact 2027 output will be delivered?

**[06:33:11.380] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006354)  
How many OFWs will benefit?

**[06:33:14.380] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006355)  
And which items are already procurement ready

**[06:33:17.380] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006356)  
or implementation ready?

**[06:33:43.780] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006364)  
Lahat Distinguished colleague, Madam Speaker,

**[06:33:47.240] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006365)  
lahat po ay implementation ready na man na po.

**[06:33:50.660] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006366)  
At ang mga servisyo caravan,

**[06:33:53.800] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006367)  
ang target po at ang kaya,

**[06:33:55.460] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006368)  
ang capacity po na pwedeng ma...

**[06:33:58.620] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006369)  
or ang...

**[06:33:59.720] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006370)  
Pwede pong mag-benefit dito ay up to 100,000 po na OFWs dahil 20 different government agencies rin po ang nagbibigay po ng servisyo.

**[06:34:20.080] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006371)  
Thank you for that, Distinguished Sponsor.

**[06:34:22.520] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006372)  
Madam Sponsor, DMW's verification fees, fee revenues are already authorized under a special account.

**[06:34:38.280] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006373)  
For fiscal year 2026, Congress has already provided the authority in the GAA. No new congressional action is needed for each release.

**[06:34:47.080] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006374)  
What is required is for DMW to submit the proper budget request and for DBM to act on it.

**[06:34:52.520] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006375)  
So, Madam Speaker, may I ask our Distinguished Colleague, how much did DMW request from DBM for the verification fee fund for fiscal year 2026?

**[06:35:07.140] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006376)  
How much was actually released and how much remains pending or unreleased?

**[06:35:22.520] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006377)  
Madam Speaker, Distinguished Colleague, para po sa verification fee fund,

**[06:35:47.440] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006378)  
na bigyan po ang DMW ng P777 million.

**[06:35:52.520] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006379)  
Para po sa P777 million, ang requested po sana nila ay P826 million.

**[06:36:01.220] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006380)  
At as far as the utilization po, 100% utilized na po ang verification fee fund and that's around P580 million po for P2026.

**[06:36:15.940] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006381)  
Kunti na lang pala po, Madam Sponsor, from the request to what was actually given.

**[06:36:22.520] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006382)  
Was there any reason why they did not give what was actually requested?

**[06:36:35.840] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006383)  
Madam Speaker, Distinguished Colleague, unfortunately, meron pong mga ceiling din na set by DBM.

**[06:36:45.300] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006384)  
Ceiling?

**[06:36:46.820] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006385)  
But if you are doing, actually, wala dapat ceiling kung 100% utilized.

**[06:36:51.840] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006386)  
You were able to utilize 100%, correct?

**[06:36:59.260] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006387)  
And these are earmarked funds specifically intended for this use.

**[06:37:04.540] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006388)  
So kung ito po ay na-utilize natin at 100%, there is no reason for DBM not to give you the requested amount.

**[06:37:15.080] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006389)  
Actually, kung na-utilize po siya bago matapos yung fiscal year,

**[06:37:19.760] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006390)  
pwede, ang alam ko, pwede po kayo mag-request ulit.

**[06:37:24.040] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006391)  
And dapat ibigay po nila yun kasi yung earmarked funds are intended for you.

**[06:37:29.300] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006392)  
Yes po. Kaya po, tumaas rin po ng P777 million po from P580 million po last year.

**[06:37:40.080] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006393)  
For 2027, P777 million naman po.

**[06:37:44.420] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006394)  
But are you aware, Madam Sponsor, that the DMW, once na-utilize,

**[06:37:49.760] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006395)  
you may request again?

**[06:37:58.220] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006396)  
Kailan po nyo na-utilize at 100%?

**[06:38:19.760] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006397)  
As of July, or sorry, August 2026 po, utilized na po.

**[06:38:27.460] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006398)  
100% utilized na po.

**[06:38:29.360] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006399)  
And currently, yung ginagamit na lang po ay yung general fund, the P380 million.

**[06:38:34.480] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006400)  
Magkano pa po ang remaining sa earmarked funds ninyo?

**[06:38:38.720] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006401)  
100% utilized na po.

**[06:38:40.300] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006402)  
No, no, no. I mean, on the fund itself.

**[06:38:46.100] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006403)  
Hindi yung nagamit nyo at saka yung binigay.

**[06:38:48.220] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006404)  
Yung account.

**[06:38:49.760] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006405)  
Nung earmarked funds yun.

**[06:39:21.060] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006423)  
Parang as of 2025, tama po ba? Ang ending balance was 5.6 billion?

**[06:39:38.440] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-006424)  
With the indulgence of our distinguished colleague and our sponsor, please wrap up.

**[06:39:44.900] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006425)  
Madam Speaker, Distinguished Colleague, currently po ang balance sa Treasury ay 4.36 billion po para po sa verification fee.

**[06:39:58.440] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006426)  
So yan po ang sinasabi natin, yung pera po under verification fees is sa inyo po yan.

**[06:40:05.620] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006427)  
And if naubos nyo na by July, yung 100%, ang sabi po ng DBM, pwede po kayo mag-request ulit.

**[06:40:14.900] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006428)  
So you still have 4.6 billion, pwede po kayo mag-request ulit para lang magamit para sa ating mga OFWs.

**[06:40:21.860] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006429)  
So in closing, Madam Speaker, our questions are intended to establish the basis for supporting these requests,

**[06:40:31.380] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006430)  
not to deprive the DMW and OWA of the resources they need.

**[06:40:37.740] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006431)  
Madam Speaker, CPBRD's figures show that DMW and OWA substantially improved their obligation and disbursement

**[06:40:44.900] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006432)  
performance in 2025. The remaining audit and implementation issues are being addressed

**[06:40:51.220] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006433)  
through stronger controls and accountability. Madam Speaker, subject to the specific outputs,

**[06:40:56.940] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006434)  
implementation schedules, safeguards, and quarterly reporting commitments placed on record,

**[06:41:02.960] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006435)  
I manifest my support for OWA's revised 2 billion request and DMW's separate 2 billion priority package.

**[06:41:10.820] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006436)  
Madam Speaker, these are not luxuries.

**[06:41:14.900] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006437)  
They are needed for emergency repatriation, welfare and legal assistance, lawyers and doctors reintegration,

**[06:41:21.840] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006438)  
the OFW hospital, safer offices, frontline overseas operations, seafarer and airport facilities,

**[06:41:29.980] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006439)  
skills upgrading, and scholarships for OFW children.

**[06:41:34.720] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006440)  
So Madam Speaker, I respectfully urge the favorable consideration of these priority requests

**[06:41:41.020] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006441)  
with the assurance that every peso must be translated.

**[06:41:44.180] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006442)  
into actual service for OFWs and their families.

**[06:41:48.180] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-006443)  
Maraming salamat po.

**[06:41:51.300] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-006444)  
Majority Leader.

**[06:41:53.440] Rep. Ryan S. Recto** (asr-006445)  
Madam Speaker, I move to recognize the Honorable Sarah Jane Elago to interpolate the sponsor.

**[06:41:59.240] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-006446)  
The Honorable Sarah Jane Elago of Gabriela Women's Party List is hereby recognized for her interpolation.

**[06:42:08.260] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006447)  
Maraming salamat Madam Speaker.

**[06:42:10.960] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006448)  
I'd like to start with the introduction.

**[06:42:14.780] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006449)  
Increase of minimum wages for Filipino domestic helpers.

**[06:42:19.940] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006450)  
In August 2025, Madam Speaker, the DMW issued Advisory No. 25, Series of 2025,

**[06:42:29.300] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006451)  
increasing the minimum wage for migrant domestic helpers from 400 U.S. dollars to 500 U.S. dollars.

**[06:42:36.760] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006452)  
Aside from incorporating the new minimum wage into the employment contracts processed by the DMW,

**[06:42:44.180] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006453)  
the advisory also directed migrant workers' offices to engage with host governments and foreign recruitment agencies

**[06:42:51.940] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006454)  
to ensure that the new minimum wage is implemented in their respective countries.

**[06:42:57.420] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006455)  
Aside from incorporating the increased minimum wage into the employment contracts processed by the DMW,

**[06:43:06.520] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006456)  
may we know from the distinguished sponsor, how does the DMW ensure that migrant workers are actually paid,

**[06:43:13.480] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006457)  
at least the minimum monthly wage mandated under Advisory No. 25?

**[06:43:28.240] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006458)  
Madam Speaker, Distinguished Colleague,

**[06:43:31.300] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006459)  
lahat po ng mga kontrata ay rinireview at meron din po tayo mga labor attaches on ground para po i-address po itong mga concerns ito.

**[06:43:43.480] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006460)  
Maraming salamat po.

**[06:43:45.280] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006461)  
At kung meron po tayong active monitoring, listahan ng mga kaso at ang status nito, ay hihingin din po ng ating representasyon.

**[06:43:53.520] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006462)  
Ngayon naman, Madam Speaker, sa iba pong mahalaga din na concern,

**[06:43:58.840] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006463)  
ang pagdademand ng child support from an OFW parent.

**[06:44:03.280] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006464)  
The Philippines has been a party to the Convention on the International Recovery of Child Support and Other Forms of Family Maintenance.

**[06:44:13.480] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006465)  
Madam Speaker, while the central authority of the Philippines for this particular convention is the Department of Social Welfare and Development,

**[06:44:22.740] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006466)  
may we know what the role of the DMW is in implementing this obligation?

**[06:44:43.480] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006467)  
Madam Speaker, Distinguished Colleague,

**[06:44:52.060] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006468)  
every time po meron claims for child support para po sa ating mga OFWs.

**[06:45:00.000] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006469)  
CMW po, sinusummon po ang ating OFW at we direct them to our legal attachés or our labor attachés para po magampanan nila yung kailangan nilang ibigay na child support.

**[06:45:20.340] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006470)  
Madam Speaker, what particular item in the budget is used for this assistance for those demanding child or spousal support from an OFW parent?

**[06:45:50.340] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006471)  
Distinguished Colleague, ito po ay nagagaling sa Labor Attachés Welfare Services Fund. Yun po nagagaling ang pondo.

**[06:46:04.680] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006472)  
Maraming salamat po. Paano naman po yung mga lugar na wala pong Migrant Workers Office? What about those jurisdictions?

**[06:46:12.720] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006473)  
How do you coordinate with the Department of Foreign Affairs and other relevant agencies to help?

**[06:46:19.660] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006474)  
With this matter.

**[06:46:23.420] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006475)  
Madam Speaker, Distinguished Colleague, we work closely po with DFA at ang ating mga embassies sa mga lugar na wala po tayong mga MWOs with their assistance to nationals units.

**[06:46:43.440] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006476)  
Maraming salamat Madam Speaker. At nasilip din po natin sa DFA na mababa yung utilization.

**[06:46:49.660] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006477)  
Nang assistance to nationals.

**[06:46:51.580] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006478)  
Samantalang sa amin din pong mga lumalapit sa ating office, marami din pong mga nangangailangan pa sana ng mga assistance.

**[06:46:59.640] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006479)  
Kaya po sana po, bukod dun sa close coordination, yung pondo na nandyan na at nakalaan para sa ganitong tipo ng mga assistance ay magamit natin para makatulong.

**[06:47:10.720] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006480)  
Now Madam Chair, under the Expanded Solo Parent Act, the spouse, family member, or guardian of the child of an OFW,

**[06:47:19.660] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006481)  
who falls under low or semi-skilled worker category, is considered a solo parent.

**[06:47:26.060] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006482)  
In supporting our migrant workers, does the DMW have programs that also assist solo parents?

**[06:47:34.760] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006483)  
Paari po ba natin malaman kung ano yung mga espesipiko na programa na ito?

**[06:47:41.820] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006484)  
Madam Speaker, Distinguished Colleague,

**[06:47:46.820] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006485)  
currently the department addresses these cases.

**[06:47:49.660] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006486)  
Through a combination of family support, livelihood, employment, and referral services.

**[06:47:56.400] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006487)  
And under Kalinga, solo parents and primary caregivers may access psychosocial and family-oriented interventions.

**[06:48:05.880] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006488)  
Under Kabuhayan, they may be matched with livelihood or employment pathways suited to their caregiving responsibilities.

**[06:48:14.040] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006489)  
Where additional social services are needed,

**[06:48:17.720] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006490)  
Kaagapay and the National Reintegration,

**[06:48:19.660] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006491)  
allow DMW to refer the solo parent to appropriate partner agencies.

**[06:48:26.360] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006492)  
So far, 78 beneficiaries po na bigyan sa 2025 at 326 beneficiaries po sa 2026.

**[06:48:36.480] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006493)  
Yan po ang mga programa.

**[06:48:38.120] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006494)  
Ang nabanggit ng mga programa, Madam Speaker, malaki din po yung hiningi ng DMW, ng OWA para sa mga ito.

**[06:48:44.320] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006495)  
Ngunit, hindi pa rin po sapat talaga dun sa laki ng pangangailangan,

**[06:48:48.600] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006496)  
yung lalaan na pondo.

**[06:48:49.920] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006497)  
Kaya, kung may pagkakataon na taasan yung pondo ng mga proyekto na ito,

**[06:48:55.200] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006498)  
ay susuportahan ng ating representasyon.

**[06:48:57.840] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006499)  
Ngayon naman po, nabanggit din po kanina ng mga nauna sa atin,

**[06:49:01.200] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006500)  
yung tungkol sa halaga ng ating mga reintegration programs.

**[06:49:05.460] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006501)  
May we know from the distinguished sponsor,

**[06:49:08.320] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006502)  
what the particular programs are of DMW and OWA,

**[06:49:13.760] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006503)  
specifically intended for older migrant workers.

**[06:49:18.600] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006504)  
We have programs that specifically address a retirement and other post-employment support.

**[06:50:05.720] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006506)  
Madam Speaker, distinguished colleague,

**[06:50:08.120] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006507)  
for older returnees, DMW combines financial literacy, livelihood support, skills transition, mentoring,

**[06:50:17.860] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006508)  
employment,

**[06:50:18.600] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006509)  
facilitation, and social protection referrals,

**[06:50:21.540] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006510)  
depending on the worker's capacity and plans.

**[06:50:25.720] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006511)  
The programs under Kaalaman can help the OFW acquire or adapt skills,

**[06:50:32.200] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006512)  
while Kabuhayan offers livelihood and entrepreneurship pathways.

**[06:50:38.340] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006513)  
FAS-SBMT, or Financial Awareness and Small Business Mentoring Training,

**[06:50:43.380] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006514)  
is also relevant for older returnees who wish to manage

**[06:50:48.340] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006515)  
savings or transition into small enterprise.

**[06:50:51.680] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006516)  
And so far, it's served 4,100 beneficiaries in 2024.

**[06:50:56.700] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006517)  
In 2025, 11,138 beneficiaries.

**[06:51:02.860] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006518)  
And so far, from January to August of 2026, 7,309 beneficiaries po ito.

**[06:51:10.980] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006519)  
At sa OWA naman po,

**[06:51:12.400] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006520)  
ito po.

**[06:51:18.340] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006521)  
At sa OWA naman po,

**[06:51:21.680] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006522)  
ang mga reintegration services rin naman nila,

**[06:51:24.600] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006523)  
nakapag-natulungan po ang 410,591 families in 2025.

**[06:51:39.140] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006524)  
OFWs and their families po.

**[06:51:41.660] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006525)  
So kasama po dyan ang Family Development Support Program,

**[06:51:45.120] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006526)  
Capacity Building for LG Europe Peso,

**[06:51:48.340] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006527)  
Enhanced EDT and Demo Farm Visitation,

**[06:51:53.140] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006528)  
iba-iba po ang mga programa.

**[06:51:56.260] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006529)  
Now, Madam Speaker, kung meron po ang ating DMW at OWA

**[06:52:00.500] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006530)  
ng detalye batay sa edad, sa kapasidad,

**[06:52:04.700] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006531)  
sa profesyon,

**[06:52:05.620] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006532)  
ay mainam po ito para makatulong po sa ating mga tinatalakay ngayon

**[06:52:10.060] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006533)  
ng mga panukalang batas.

**[06:52:12.020] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006534)  
Yung isa dyan ay yung pagpapalakas ng mga reintegration program.

**[06:52:15.860] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006535)  
Pero meron po dyan isang probesyon na,

**[06:52:18.340] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006536)  
hindi pa naisama, na sa tingin ko po ay isa sa mga panawagan

**[06:52:22.760] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006537)  
ng ating mga bumalik na mga OFWs.

**[06:52:26.260] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006538)  
May we know, Madam Speaker, what the positions of DMW and OWA are

**[06:52:31.060] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006539)  
on establishing a retirement fund for OFWs

**[06:52:35.840] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006540)  
who are no longer able to work

**[06:52:38.120] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006541)  
or have reached a certain age at which they can no longer perform their jobs?

**[06:52:48.340] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006542)  
Madam Speaker, Distinguished Colleague,

**[06:53:01.880] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006543)  
currently po ang DMW nagpapartner po with SSS

**[06:53:10.180] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006544)  
para po makapag-establish ang mga pension funds

**[06:53:14.540] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006545)  
para po sa ating mga retiring OFWs.

**[06:53:17.820] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006546)  
And at the same time,

**[06:53:18.340] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006547)  
they are also helping develop legislation

**[06:53:21.340] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006548)  
para po may sa batas na ang ating retirement funds for our OFWs.

**[06:53:30.040] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006549)  
Ito ay sinusuportahan ng buong-buo ng DMW at OWA, Madam Speaker.

**[06:53:35.460] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006550)  
Yes.

**[06:53:36.780] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006551)  
At ngayon naman, Madam Speaker,

**[06:53:38.840] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006552)  
napakahalaga rin po nito sa atin

**[06:53:40.500] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006553)  
ang mabalitaan tayo tungkol sa implementation

**[06:53:42.760] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006554)  
ng Magna Carta for seafarers.

**[06:53:47.360] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006555)  
Yes.

**[06:53:47.440] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006556)  
Has DMW started implementing the Magna Carta?

**[06:53:51.640] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006557)  
If yes, what has DMW changed in its policies and regulations

**[06:53:57.340] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006558)  
because of this Magna Carta?

**[06:54:00.460] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006559)  
May we also know what the challenges are

**[06:54:04.640] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006560)  
in terms of the implementation of this Magna Carta,

**[06:54:08.200] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006561)  
especially now that we're in the first year of its implementation?

**[06:54:17.440] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006562)  
Madam Speaker,

**[06:54:21.580] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006563)  
Distinguished Colleague,

**[06:54:25.140] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006564)  
so far po ang DMW nag-issue po ng sea-based

**[06:54:29.220] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006565)  
or nag-update po ng pag-issue ng sea-based rules

**[06:54:32.960] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006566)  
at na-standardize yun po ang mga kontrata

**[06:54:35.400] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006567)  
ng ating mga seafarers at the same time,

**[06:54:38.460] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006568)  
ang fisher folks po.

**[06:54:40.480] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006569)  
Nag-update, ibig sabihin po,

**[06:54:42.840] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006570)  
ongoing yung ating shipping?

**[06:54:46.140] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006571)  
Yeah.

**[06:54:47.440] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006572)  
Issued last June po.

**[06:54:49.220] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006573)  
It was issued June this year?

**[06:54:51.660] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006574)  
Yes.

**[06:54:52.200] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006575)  
Alright.

**[06:54:52.580] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006576)  
And has already been issued.

**[06:54:54.860] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006577)  
Alright.

**[06:54:55.680] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006578)  
Now, mangingin na lang po tayo ng ulat,

**[06:54:58.740] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006579)  
ng pinakauling ulat dito ng DMW

**[06:55:01.860] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006580)  
kung kumusta na ang ating Magna Carta.

**[06:55:05.620] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006581)  
We will provide the report.

**[06:55:07.220] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006582)  
Yes.

**[06:55:09.160] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006583)  
Nais ko lang din pong maibahagi sa ating kapulungan.

**[06:55:13.080] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006584)  
Naging vocal din po ang aming representation

**[06:55:15.460] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006585)  
mula sa Gabriela Women's Party.

**[06:55:17.440] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006586)  
Tungkol sa provision ng Magna Carta na ito,

**[06:55:21.340] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006587)  
kung saan po yung monetary reward

**[06:55:24.240] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006588)  
of the seafarers are put in escrow

**[06:55:27.480] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006589)  
pending the finality of judgment

**[06:55:29.400] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006590)  
which can take years.

**[06:55:31.420] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006591)  
So, that particular provision,

**[06:55:34.200] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006592)  
sa tingin po natin,

**[06:55:35.560] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006593)  
dapat ding ma-review ng kapulungan na ito.

**[06:55:39.360] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006594)  
At makakatulong po yung inyong implementation so far

**[06:55:43.520] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006595)  
para makita ba natin kung dapat po itong tanggalin,

**[06:55:47.440] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006596)  
dito sa batas na ito.

**[06:55:52.640] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006597)  
Ngayon naman, Madam Speaker,

**[06:55:55.000] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006598)  
tungkol po sa naiulat na blacklisting

**[06:55:58.940] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006599)  
ng mga seafarers

**[06:56:00.900] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006600)  
kapag sila po ay nag-a-avail ng kanilang right to refuse sailing.

**[06:56:05.640] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006601)  
Nakarating po sa ating office na kapag sila daw po,

**[06:56:08.900] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006602)  
dalawang bagay, no.

**[06:56:11.280] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006603)  
Una, may mga pagkakataon daw po

**[06:56:13.940] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006604)  
na hindi nila ma-exercise itong kanilang karapatan,

**[06:56:17.440] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006605)  
ng kanilang right to refuse sailing.

**[06:56:18.380] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006606)  
Dahil pag majority daw po ng mga seafarer

**[06:56:21.820] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006607)  
ay bumoto na tumuloy pa rin,

**[06:56:23.700] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006608)  
ay tumutuloy pa rin sila

**[06:56:25.000] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006609)  
na hindi po nasasangalang yung kanilang karapatan na tumanggi.

**[06:56:32.220] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006610)  
Ngayon, yung pangalawa naman po,

**[06:56:34.540] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006611)  
ay kapag sila ay nag-a-avail,

**[06:56:36.500] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006612)  
nag-exercise po ng kanilang right to refuse sailing,

**[06:56:38.980] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006613)  
pagbalik po nila dito sa Pilipinas at aalis na,

**[06:56:42.100] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006614)  
ay nalalaman po nila na sila ay blacklisted na.

**[06:56:46.320] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006615)  
Meron pong,

**[06:56:47.440] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006616)  
statement ang DMW tungkol dito

**[06:56:51.260] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006617)  
at wini-welcome naman po ng ating representation.

**[06:56:54.680] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006618)  
The DMW called for Filipino seafarers

**[06:56:57.460] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006619)  
who refuse to sail to high-risk areas

**[06:57:00.400] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006620)  
not to be discriminated against in future employment.

**[06:57:05.220] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006621)  
They must also receive proper compensation

**[06:57:07.580] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006622)  
and free repatriation to the Philippines.

**[06:57:11.540] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006623)  
May we know po what the DMW has done so far.

**[06:57:17.440] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006624)  
To ensure that the Filipinos won't be discriminated against

**[06:57:22.620] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006625)  
when they exercise their right to refuse sailing.

**[06:57:27.460] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006626)  
Especially po in high-risk areas now.

**[06:57:29.700] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006627)  
Thank you.

**[06:57:45.280] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006628)  
Madam Speaker,

**[06:57:46.360] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006629)  
Distinguished Colleague,

**[06:57:47.980] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006630)  
So for the manning agencies na meron pong mga violations,

**[06:57:52.080] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006631)  
DMW would subject them to an investigation

**[06:57:57.360] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006632)  
and also subject them to violation fees.

**[06:58:06.080] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006633)  
Madam Speaker,

**[06:58:06.580] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006634)  
pag meron ba silang violation,

**[06:58:08.020] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006635)  
ano pong mangyayari?

**[06:58:09.000] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006636)  
Bawal na po silang pag-operate?

**[06:58:10.880] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006637)  
Masasara po ba?

**[06:58:12.260] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006638)  
Masususpend muna?

**[06:58:16.360] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006639)  
Madam Speaker,

**[06:58:21.460] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006640)  
Distinguished Colleague,

**[06:58:23.260] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006641)  
Pwede po masuspend ang manning agency

**[06:58:26.500] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006642)  
at kung paulit-ulit po

**[06:58:28.500] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006643)  
or patuloy pa rin po ang violation,

**[06:58:30.960] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006644)  
pwede pong makancel po ang kanilang lisensya.

**[06:58:33.580] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006645)  
Madam Speaker,

**[06:58:35.060] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006646)  
ano po yung basis po natin

**[06:58:37.360] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006647)  
ng ganitong tipo ng mga

**[06:58:38.940] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006648)  
prov, penalties,

**[06:58:41.700] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006649)  
standards at saka yung graduated penalties po

**[06:58:45.920] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006650)  
na nabanggit po ninyo.

**[06:58:48.360] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006651)  
Saan po natin yan makikita?

**[06:59:01.000] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006652)  
Madam Speaker,

**[06:59:02.340] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006653)  
distinguished colleagues,

**[06:59:04.580] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006654)  
ito po ay base sa

**[06:59:05.840] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006655)  
sea-based rules po na

**[06:59:07.820] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006656)  
in-issue natin noong

**[06:59:09.560] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006657)  
June 2026 lang po.

**[06:59:12.340] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006658)  
And so far,

**[06:59:13.100] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006659)  
1,300 po

**[06:59:16.420] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006660)  
Filipino seafarers

**[06:59:17.980] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006661)  
refused to sail

**[06:59:20.080] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006662)  
or traverse.

**[06:59:21.160] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006663)  
Ito po ang so far.

**[06:59:24.920] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006664)  
Maraming salamat po.

**[06:59:27.180] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006665)  
Bukod po sa ganyang information,

**[06:59:28.640] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006666)  
nahihingin din po natin sa ating

**[06:59:30.360] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006667)  
mga departamento, nandito rin po

**[06:59:33.120] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006668)  
yung ulat na

**[06:59:34.380] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006669)  
yung karapatan po ng mga seafarer

**[06:59:37.240] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006670)  
na malaman kung saan po yung

**[06:59:39.100] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006671)  
ruta na dadaanan ng kanilang

**[06:59:40.920] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006672)  
mga vessels. Marami daw po

**[06:59:42.840] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006673)  
hindi binibigay sa kanilang information

**[06:59:45.020] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006674)  
till nakasakay na sila.

**[06:59:47.340] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006675)  
In fact po, nakatanggap din po

**[06:59:49.360] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006676)  
tayo na balita na

**[06:59:50.740] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006677)  
yung

**[06:59:51.920] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006678)  
masakit po sa loob na

**[06:59:55.120] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006679)  
namatay, nasi-farers

**[06:59:57.000] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006680)  
na taga Calamba, Laguna,

**[06:59:59.660] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006681)  
hindi po

**[07:00:00.000] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006682)  
kung nila alam ang ruta ng barko, nasa war zones, nasa high-risk areas po pala dadaan.

**[07:00:05.180] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006683)  
So yung ganito pong karapatan ng ating mga seafarer,

**[07:00:08.160] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006684)  
dapat matiyak po natin na masasakatuparan at hindi pangarap lamang para sa ating mga seafarers.

**[07:00:18.240] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006685)  
Madam Speaker, Distinguished Colleague,

**[07:00:21.200] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006686)  
kung meron pong violations or kung meron pong mga sitwasyon na hindi po alam ng ating seafarer kung totoong ruta,

**[07:00:30.000] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006687)  
ng kanilang pupuntuhan, pwede pong isubject po ng DMW into investigation, again, ang violators po.

**[07:00:40.600] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006688)  
Ngayon, Madam Speaker, narinig po natin yung mga nag-exercise ng kanilang right to refuse sailing.

**[07:00:46.580] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006689)  
Pwede niyo po ba kami i-update kung sa ngayon po, yung mga seafarers na gustong makauwi, ay nakauwi na po ba?

**[07:00:54.940] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006690)  
Ilang request po yung inyong natanggap?

**[07:00:57.980] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006691)  
Tapos po, yung bilang din po ng mga seafarers na nakauwi na,

**[07:01:05.020] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006692)  
ingin po sana ng representation na ito yung breakdown.

**[07:01:09.800] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006693)  
Pagkatapos po ng ating deliberation, ay yung hapon.

**[07:01:12.420] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006694)  
Yes, we will oblige.

**[07:01:14.620] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006695)  
Maraming maraming salamat po, Madam Speaker.

**[07:01:16.760] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006696)  
At muli, ang representation po na ito ay sumusuporta sa pagdadagdag ng pondo para sa Action Fund.

**[07:01:25.420] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006697)  
Noway yung mga hindi pa po nakakakuha.

**[07:01:27.440] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006698)  
Ang kanilang mga Action Fund ay makakuha na.

**[07:01:29.660] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006699)  
Alam ko po, hindi madali ang sitwasyon, hindi lang dito sa bansa, kundi sa pandaig dito.

**[07:01:36.040] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006700)  
Kaya nga, kinakailangan at maasam po natin, habang tinataasan yung suporta para sa mga migrant workers,

**[07:01:43.180] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006701)  
dapat higit natin tinataasan yung suporta para sa disenting trabaho dito sa Pilipinas,

**[07:01:49.400] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006702)  
nakabubuhay na sahod para sa ating mga manggagawang Pilipinas.

**[07:01:52.860] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-006703)  
Maraming maraming salamat, Madam Speaker. Maraming salamat sa ating distinguished sponsor.

**[07:01:57.440] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006704)  
Maraming salamat.

**[07:02:01.480] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-006705)  
Majority Leader.

**[07:02:03.300] Rep. Ryan S. Recto** (asr-006706)  
Madam Speaker, I move to recognize the Honorable Dada Kiramizmula to interpolate the sponsor.

**[07:02:09.320] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-006707)  
The Honorable Dada Kiramizmula of Partilistak Bayan is hereby recognized for her interpolation.

**[07:02:17.180] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006708)  
Maraming salamat, Madam Speaker, Madam Sponsor.

**[07:02:20.480] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006709)  
Assalamualaikum warahmatullahi wabarakatuhu.

**[07:02:23.800] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006710)  
I have a few questions on the mass deportations.

**[07:02:27.440] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006711)  
of Filipinos in the United States.

**[07:02:30.780] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006712)  
Madam Speaker, distinguished sponsor, my office has received reports

**[07:02:35.340] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006713)  
ng ating mga Filipino seafarers na under sa Magsaysay Maritime Corporation

**[07:02:42.200] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006714)  
at nagtatrabaho sa Princess Cruises Limited ay sinearch ng walang warrant.

**[07:02:50.260] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006715)  
Kinukuha ang kanilang mga gamit gaya ng cellphone

**[07:02:53.520] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006716)  
at inaakusahan ng krimen ng walang due process at ebidensya.

**[07:02:57.440] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006717)  
Sa parehong linggo, sila ay dinedeport, pabalik sa Pilipinas.

**[07:03:06.620] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006718)  
Paulit-ulit na nangyayari ito bilang bahagi ng operational tidal wave ng Trump government sa US.

**[07:03:15.500] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006719)  
Madam Speaker, Madam Sponsor, what is the DMW's response to the mass deportations of Filipino seafarers

**[07:03:24.540] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006720)  
in the United States under operational tidal wave?

**[07:03:27.440] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006721)  
Madam Speaker, distinguished colleague, habang rinerespeto po ng DMW ang sovereignty at mga batas ng ibang bansa,

**[07:03:42.600] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006722)  
nananatiling pong committed ang ating Philippine government para protektahan ang mga karapatan,

**[07:03:49.980] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006723)  
ang welfare and due process ng ating mga Filipino seafarers.

**[07:03:54.700] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006724)  
Lalong-lalo na po sa mga ganitong sitwasyon.

**[07:03:57.440] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006725)  
We must also distinguish among an allegation, an immigration action, and a criminal filing.

**[07:04:07.240] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006726)  
The department will not prejudge the cases of our returning seafarers,

**[07:04:12.200] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006727)  
particularly when no criminal charge or conviction has been established.

**[07:04:18.360] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006728)  
The DMW's responsibility is to ensure that affected workers receive appropriate assistance,

**[07:04:24.660] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006729)  
that their cases are properly documented,

**[07:04:27.440] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006730)  
and that the necessary coordination is undertaken with the relevant Philippine and foreign authorities,

**[07:04:35.160] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006731)  
consistent with the applicable laws and procedures.

**[07:04:39.420] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006732)  
We have also consulted the licensed manning agencies and their deported seafarers and provided them with assistance.

**[07:04:48.400] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006733)  
Maraming salamat po dito.

**[07:04:50.280] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006734)  
Paano po kaya nakikipag-ugnayan ang DMW sa Philippine Embassy sa US

**[07:04:55.880] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006735)  
para matiyak na may legal aid ang ating mga kababayan?

**[07:05:01.560] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006736)  
Patuloy po ang pakikipag-ugnayan ng DMW with the Department of Foreign Affairs

**[07:05:08.500] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006737)  
sa ating mga migrant workers offices at sa mga kaukulang US authorities

**[07:05:13.800] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006738)  
upang mas maunawaan ang mga pangyayari at circumstances surrounding the removals.

**[07:05:21.660] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006739)  
Tinitiyak din po natin na ang mga affected seafarers ay,

**[07:05:25.880] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006740)  
maayos na nare-refer para sa kinakailangan mga welfare, legal, at iba pang mga assistance.

**[07:05:34.720] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006741)  
Yun na po, ilan na po yan sa mga assistance at support na kaya pong ibigay ng DMW.

**[07:05:43.180] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006742)  
Maraming salamat po Madam Speaker, Madam Sponsor.

**[07:05:46.960] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006743)  
Nakakalungkot no na we've received reports from some of the seafarers.

**[07:05:52.020] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006744)  
Actually, dumaan po sila sa aking opisina kamakailan.

**[07:05:55.880] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006745)  
Who were detained before deportation that when they called the Philippine Embassy to the US,

**[07:06:02.140] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006746)  
the response was, okay lang yan, makakalaya ka naman, diba?

**[07:06:09.660] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006747)  
Does DMW share the same opinion?

**[07:06:25.880] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006748)  
Madam Speaker, Distinguished Colleague, currently po tinatrabaho po ito ng DMW

**[07:06:32.580] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006749)  
para po ma-make sure, or coordinated with, of course, the Manning Agency

**[07:06:39.000] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006750)  
para po meron pang, merong legal assistance po, supports pa lang.

**[07:06:46.900] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006751)  
Yun po ang aksyon na ginagawa ng DMW.

**[07:06:49.600] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006752)  
Maraming salamat at naniniwala ako sa DMW na ito'y tutugunan nila.

**[07:06:54.760] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006753)  
Nakakalungkot.

**[07:06:55.520] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006754)  
Kasi currently, nasa mahigit dalawang daang seafarers na po ang nagkaroon ng ganitong problema.

**[07:07:03.300] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006755)  
Yung tinatawag nilang operational tidal wave.

**[07:07:07.740] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006756)  
Kamakailan ay naglabas na po sila ng kanilang press release patungkol dito sa nangyari sa kanila.

**[07:07:15.620] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006757)  
Kasi wala po eh, walang nagre-report, hindi po ito lumalabas sa media.

**[07:07:19.400] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006758)  
At ang masaklap pa, sasabihan po sila ng ating embahada na nasa US na,

**[07:07:25.520] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006759)  
okay lang yan, makakalaya ka naman.

**[07:07:29.220] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006760)  
Saklap, di ba?

**[07:07:30.680] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006761)  
Umalis ako ng aking bansa, pupunta ako para maging isang seafarer,

**[07:07:36.020] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006762)  
malalaman ko na lang na ako'y ipapababa at ako'y ipapadeport,

**[07:07:40.140] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006763)  
pero idedetain pa ako at popusasan pa ako ng ilang oras, no?

**[07:07:45.480] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006764)  
So, sana po ay matugunan po natin ito sa lalong madaling panahon, Madam Speaker, Madam Sponsor.

**[07:07:55.520] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006765)  
Madam Speaker, Madam Sponsor, magkano po kaya ang na-dispersed na mula sa Action Fund

**[07:08:01.740] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006766)  
para sa mga Pilipinong biktima ng mass deportations na walang due process?

**[07:08:09.060] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006767)  
Madam Speaker, Distinguished Colleague, so far 222 seafarers have been deported

**[07:08:16.800] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006768)  
and each were given 50,000 pesos in financial assistance under the Action Fund.

**[07:08:24.980] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006769)  
So, total po, 11.1 million po ang na-dispersed galing sa Action Fund.

**[07:08:32.300] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006770)  
Maraming salamat po.

**[07:08:33.860] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006771)  
My office also received reports that there are instances wherein six months since the seafarer has returned to the Philippines,

**[07:08:43.820] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006772)  
hindi pa rin narirelease ang Action Fund sa nasabing seafarers.

**[07:08:47.920] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006773)  
Madam Sponsor, what is the reason kaya behind this?

**[07:08:54.980] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006774)  
Madam Speaker, Distinguished Colleague, again, the reasoning behind the delay is because of the Middle East tensions

**[07:09:01.640] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006775)  
and unfortunately po yun ang nag-cause ng delay but right now, DMW is catching up

**[07:09:07.600] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006776)  
and we're committed to going back to the 30-day disbursement po ng assistance for our OFWs.

**[07:09:18.020] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006777)  
Just to put into record lang, Madam Speaker, Madam Sponsor, can the DMW commit to ensure

**[07:09:24.820] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006778)  
the expedient release of, or hindi lalagpas ng 30 days from today po?

**[07:09:32.240] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006779)  
Yes po, makakaasa po kayo.

**[07:09:33.940] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006780)  
Maraming salamat po, no?

**[07:09:35.120] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006781)  
And finally, can the Distinguished Sponsor explain to us po, ano itong tinatawag nilang Abu Dhabi Dialogue at bakit ito mahalaga?

**[07:10:10.380] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006782)  
Madam Speaker, Distinguished Colleague, the Abu Dhabi Dialogue was established in 2028

**[07:10:17.660] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006783)  
at ito po ay isang voluntary inter-government consultative forum

**[07:10:23.220] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006784)  
linking Asian countries of labor origin and destination

**[07:10:27.500] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006785)  
and it aims to enable safe, orderly, and regular migration. Currently po, mayroon po siyang 10 member states including the Gulf States po.

**[07:10:41.280] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006786)  
At magkano po kaya ang nakalaan pondo para dito?

**[07:10:54.800] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006787)  
Madam Speaker, Distinguished Colleague, currently wala po siyang allotment sa NEP.

**[07:11:01.320] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006788)  
That's why nag-manifest po ang DMW ng kanilang request for 60 million pesos po

**[07:11:07.800] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006789)  
to host at least three conferences po para sa Abu Dhabi Dialogue.

**[07:11:18.300] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006790)  
60 million pesos.

**[07:11:20.140] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006791)  
And ang Pilipinas ang host po para dito.

**[07:11:23.200] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006792)  
Tama po ba?

**[07:11:24.520] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006793)  
Yes.

**[07:11:25.260] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006795)  
Kakalungkot no na host pa naman tayo pero wala tayong pondo para dito at napaka-importante po pala nito.

**[07:11:32.980] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006796)  
Sinusuportahan ng representasyong ito ang paglalaan ng pondo para sa Abu Dhabi Dialogue.

**[07:11:39.640] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006797)  
Mahalaga ang pondong ito upang maipagpatuloy ang mga programa para sa pagsasanay,

**[07:11:46.600] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006798)  
proteksyon at mas maayos na kapakanan ng ating mga OFWs at iba pang migrant workers sa Middle East.

**[07:11:54.800] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006799)  
And in closing, Madam Speaker, Madam Sponsor, thank you for yielding into my questions.

**[07:12:02.100] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006800)  
Nais kong muling ipahayag ang aking panawagan noong budget briefing na sa gitna ng umiiral na global conflict,

**[07:12:10.340] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006801)  
mas higit nating kailangan ang matatag na DMW at OWA na may sapat na pondo upang matugunan ang pangangailangan ng ating mga overseas Filipino workers.

**[07:12:23.480] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006802)  
I therefore manifest...

**[07:12:24.800] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006803)  
I express my strong support for increasing the budget of the DMW and OWA,

**[07:12:30.560] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006804)  
particularly with the repatriation program and assistance programs to distressed OFWs,

**[07:12:37.680] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006805)  
with the guarantee na masiguro ng DMW ang mabilis, maayos at sapat na tulong sa ating mga kababayang OFWs sa oras ng pangangailangan.

**[07:12:49.960] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006806)  
Nakikita natin ang epekto pag nagagawa ng isang ahensya,

**[07:12:53.880] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006807)  
ang kanilang mandato, which is evident sa mga nagagawa ng ating DMW Region 9,

**[07:13:01.700] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006808)  
gaya ng pagtatayo ng First Regional OFW Airport Lounge outside na IA at First Regional Dedicated OFW Ward na sana ay magawa rin sa iba pang mga regyon.

**[07:13:15.300] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006809)  
Naniniwala ako na sa gabay at tinig ng ating distinguished sponsor, this August Chamber will favorably...

**[07:13:23.880] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006810)  
consider and strengthen the department's budget, particularly for these vital programs.

**[07:13:30.380] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006811)  
Maraming salamat po, Madam Speaker. Maraming salamat, Madam Sponsor.

**[07:13:34.660] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-006812)  
Majority Leader.

**[07:13:37.420] Rep. Arnan C. Panaligan** (asr-006813)  
Madam Speaker, I move that we recognize Honorable Iris Montes, 4K Partylist, to interpelate on the proposed budget of the DMW.

**[07:13:51.500] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-006814)  
The Honorable Montes is recognized for her interpelation.

**[07:13:53.880] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-006815)  
Thank you, Madam Chair.

**[07:13:56.560] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-006816)  
Ilang katanungan lamang po mula sa kababaihan, kabalikatan para sa kapakanan at kaunlaran ng bayan.

**[07:14:03.920] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-006817)  
Women consistently account for the majority of newly deployed land-based overseas Filipino workers,

**[07:14:11.060] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-006818)  
predominantly in domestic care work, a sector inherently prone to isolation, abuse, and labor exploitation.

**[07:14:20.020] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-006819)  
Under the proposed 2027 budget,

**[07:14:23.260] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-006820)  
how much of the agarang kalinga at saklolo para sa mga OFW na nangangailangan or action fund is explicitly sub-allocated

**[07:14:33.840] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-006821)  
for immediate legal intervention, medical treatment, and physical rescue of abused female domestic workers?

**[07:14:52.500] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006824)  
Madam Speaker, Distinguished Colleague, as of now po, ang action fund ay ready po na ma-

**[07:15:00.000] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006825)  
The entire fund is ready to be used for any cases para po sa kahit sinong OFW na nangangailangan.

**[07:15:08.980] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006826)  
So far po, over 50% ay actually nabigay po para sa ating mga kababaihan na OFWs.

**[07:15:16.900] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-006827)  
50% po yung halos ang naibigay mo.

**[07:15:19.880] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-006828)  
More than 50%.

**[07:15:20.640] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-006829)  
More than. Kasi mas marami nga po sila.

**[07:15:23.320] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-006830)  
I remember during the last budget hearing, I specifically requested po talaga sa DMW to have a sex disaggregated data.

**[07:15:35.100] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-006831)  
Isa po yan sa concern ko na talagang dapat ma-disaggregate yung data para alam po natin at magkano din po ang ilalaan.

**[07:15:43.000] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-006832)  
Kasi marami po talaga sila para natutugunan po natin yung pangangailangan nila.

**[07:15:49.200] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-006833)  
My next question po, under RA 9710.

**[07:15:53.320] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-006834)  
or the Magna Carta of Women, government agencies are required to protect women in specially difficult circumstances.

**[07:16:00.660] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-006835)  
Out of the total operational budget of the migrant workers' offices and runaway shelters overseas,

**[07:16:08.880] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-006836)  
what specific line item is allocated to ensure that these shelters have trauma-informed female personnel,

**[07:16:19.340] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-006837)  
dedicated mental health professionals,

**[07:16:22.640] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-006838)  
and gender...

**[07:16:23.320] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-006839)  
gender-sensitive facilities?

**[07:16:25.480] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-006840)  
Siguro po, kahit including mga gender-sensitive professionals din po, like yung mga gynecologists and the like.

**[07:16:47.800] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006841)  
Madam Speaker, Distinguished Colleague,

**[07:16:50.660] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006842)  
ang pondo po na ginagamit,

**[07:16:52.160] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006843)  
or specific line item po ay ang ating Migrant Workers Resource Centers at ang ating Labor Attachés Fund.

**[07:17:03.500] Rep. Irene G. Labadlabad** (asr-006844)  
Yun po ang pinanggagalingan.

**[07:17:05.940] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-006845)  
Maraming salamat po, Madam Speaker, Distinguished Sponsor.

**[07:17:10.880] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-006846)  
Uso po ngayon, ang dami po ngayong mga digital job scams and human trafficking rings continue to target young, vulnerable women,

**[07:17:21.320] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-006847)

**[07:17:35.520] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-006849)

Madam Speaker, Distinguished Colleague,

Thank you, Madam Chair, Distinguished Sponsor.

**[07:18:56.900] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-006862)

Thank you, Madam Speaker.

**[07:35:38.000] Rep. Ryan S. Recto** (asr-007048)  
Madam Speaker.

**[07:35:39.780] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-007049)  
Majority Leader.

**[07:35:41.580] Rep. Ryan S. Recto** (asr-007050)  
Madam Speaker, I move that we recognize

**[07:35:43.720] Rep. Ryan S. Recto** (asr-007051)  
the Honorable Dada Kiram Ismula

**[07:35:45.540] Rep. Ryan S. Recto** (asr-007052)  
to interpolate the sponsor.

**[07:35:48.080] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-007053)  
The Honorable Dada Kiram Ismula

**[07:35:49.640] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-007054)  
of Party-list Akbayan

**[07:35:50.960] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-007055)  
is hereby recognized

**[07:35:54.140] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-007056)  
for her interpolation.

**[07:35:59.280] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-007057)  
Asalamu Alaikum Warahmatullahi Wabarakatuhu.

**[07:36:04.720] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-007058)  
Madam Speaker,

**[07:36:05.820] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-007059)  
good sponsor,

**[07:36:08.920] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-007060)  
since karamihan ng mga ancestral domains

**[07:36:11.820] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-007061)  
ay nasa mga regions,

**[07:36:14.020] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-007062)  
my plano bang investment

**[07:36:14.960] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-007063)  
sa mga regional

**[07:36:16.500] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-007064)  
or field offices

**[07:36:18.040] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-007065)  
sa 2027?

**[07:36:38.140] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007071)  
Madam Speaker, yes, since most ancestral domains and ICCs/IPs are located in the regions, we recognize that NCIP's capacity must also be strongest at the field level.

**[07:36:56.700] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007072)  
For 2027, our direction is to strengthen our regional, provincial, and community service centers by providing the necessary technical personnel, field resources, and operational support.

**[07:37:13.780] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007073)  
Marami po sa trabaho ng NCIP ay ginagawa mismo sa communities.

**[07:37:20.380] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007074)  
Tulad po ng ancestral domain, delineation, census, and validation.

**[07:37:26.120] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007075)  
This requires our personnel to actually reach the communities, including those in geographically isolated and disadvantaged areas.

**[07:37:45.900] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007076)  
So, strengthening the field offices is not simply an administrative investment.

**[07:37:51.840] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007077)  
It directly increases our capacity.

**[07:37:56.120] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007078)  
To deliver the mandate of NCIP, where our ICCs, IPs, actually are.

**[07:38:04.040] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007079)  
Madam Speaker, we have submitted our Tier 2 proposal for creation of 10 provincial offices and 36 community service centers.

**[07:38:15.980] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007080)  
Unfortunately, it was not included in the 2027 NEP of NCIP.

**[07:38:26.120] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-007081)  
Madam Speaker, good sponsor.

**[07:38:29.080] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-007082)  
Sa NEP po ng 2027, nakasaad na ang 2025 GAA, target para sa percentage increase ng ancestral domains or lands recognized and delineated ay 11%.

**[07:38:44.220] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-007083)  
Ngunit ang actual performance ay 0%.

**[07:38:48.260] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-007084)  
Sa 2026, target muli ay 11%.

**[07:38:52.140] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-007085)  
As of today, ilang percent na po ba ang na-accomplish?

**[07:38:56.020] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007087)  
It is a target based on the Actual Cases Program.

**[07:39:06.260] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007088)  
There is states of processing, complexity, location, and the resources available for implementation.

**[07:39:14.020] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007089)  
At the same time, the 113 represents only the funded annual target.

**[07:39:19.980] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007090)  
It does not represent the total need or the total number of

**[07:39:26.420] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007092)  
ancestral domain claims that NCIP still has to process.

**[07:39:32.060] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007093)  
Kaya po, ang gusto namin ipakita sa Congress is the distinction between the funded target and the actual mandate.

**[07:39:43.200] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007094)  
The NCIP will deliver what is funded while continuing to present the remaining requirements so that, over time, it can address the larger backlog.

**[07:39:56.020] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007095)  
The target should be realistic enough to be accountable.

**[07:39:59.460] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007096)  
But the budget should also progressively respond to the actual universe of ancestral domains' claims that remain to be served.

**[07:40:11.380] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007097)  
Madam Speaker,

**[07:40:13.200] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007098)  
CAGTI and CALTI processing is the legal recognition of ancestral domain and ancestral land rights.

**[07:40:24.140] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007099)  
It is not an ordinary process.

**[07:40:26.020] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007100)  
It is not an ordinary permit or simple administrative transactions.

**[07:40:31.680] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007101)  
Ang kinikilala po natin dito ay karapatan sa lupa na may intergenerational consequences.

**[07:40:41.040] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007102)  
Once recognized, it affects not only the present generation but also the generations that will come after them.

**[07:40:50.000] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007103)  
That is why the process has to be thorough and evidence-based.

**[07:40:56.020] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007104)  
It involves community participation, genealogical and historical research, census, survey and mapping, validation, resolution of boundary and overlapping claims, legal review, and ultimately, commission deliberations.

**[07:41:17.040] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007105)  
May mga pagkakataon din na kailangan ng coordination with DNR, DAR, LRA.

**[07:41:26.020] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007106)  
LGUs and other agencies, because land records, existing titles, surveys,

**[07:41:34.020] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007114)  
And other claims have to be reconciled.

**[07:41:38.500] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-007115)  
Madam Speaker, Good Sponsor, ulitin ko po yung tanong ko.

**[07:41:42.320] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-007116)  
Ilang percent na ang na-accomplish na ADs and ALs ngayong 2026 po?

**[07:41:51.960] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-007117)  
Or as of today?

**[07:42:04.020] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007118)  
Madam Speaker, the 11% is the annual target based on the resources provided for 2027.

**[07:42:17.880] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007119)  
It does not represent the total number of ancestral domains and lands that still need to be processed.

**[07:42:27.320] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007120)  
The 11% corresponds to the 30 ancestral domains or lands.

**[07:42:34.020] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007121)  
For many years, the budget of NCIP has not been proportionate to the actual universe of its mandate.

**[07:42:44.820] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007122)  
Kaya po, ang target ay nakabatay sa kung ano ang kanyang pondohan, hindi sa kabuhuang pangangailangan.

**[07:42:54.940] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007123)  
This is precisely why NCIP proposed a higher budget.

**[07:43:00.660] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007124)  
So we can progressively increase our coverage.

**[07:43:04.020] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007125)  
And process more ancestral domains and land claims.

**[07:43:10.200] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-007126)  
Sige po, Madam Speaker, Good Sponsor, baguhin ko na lang ang tanong ko.

**[07:43:15.780] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-007127)  
Ilan po ang na-accomplish na ng NCIP para mabigyan ng CalTee at CADTee for 2026?

**[07:43:28.140] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007128)  
For 2026 po, ang approved is tatlo pong CAD-T at

**[07:43:34.020] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007129)  
CAL-T.

**[07:43:38.480] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007130)  
Ang total po ay 529.

**[07:43:46.580] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-007131)  
Approved, CADTee and CalTee.

**[07:43:50.740] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007132)  
Approved po, approved.

**[07:43:52.980] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-007133)  
Maraming salamat po, Madam Speaker, Good Sponsor.

**[07:43:56.900] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-007134)  
Sa NEP po ba ng 2027, target ulit po ay 11%.

**[07:44:01.900] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-007135)  
Ilan po ang tiyak na bilang ng ancestral domain na sakop ng target na ito?

**[07:44:09.540] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-007136)  
At bakit po taon-taon 11% lamang ang target ng NCIP?

**[07:44:18.420] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007137)  
Sa 2027 po, Madam Speaker, ay 74 for 2027 ang target.

**[07:44:30.000] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-007138)  
At bakit po kaya taon-taon?

**[07:44:31.880] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-007139)  
At bakit po kaya taon-taon 11% ang target natin?

**[07:44:39.520] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007140)  
Madam Speaker, yung 11% po is the annual target based on the resources provided for 2027.

**[07:44:51.080] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007141)  
It does not represent the total number of ancestral domains and lands that still need to be processed.

**[07:44:58.500] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007142)  
The 11%

**[07:45:00.638] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007143)  
corresponds to 30 ancestral domain or land claims.

**[07:45:07.640] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007144)  
For many years, the budget for NCIP has not been proportionate to the actual universe of its mandate.

**[07:45:16.260] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007145)  
Kaya po, ang target ay nakabatay sa kung ano ang kanyang pondohan, hindi sa kabuang pangangailangan.

**[07:45:25.000] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-007146)  
Naintindihan ko po ito, Madam Speaker, Good Sponsor.

**[07:45:30.180] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-007147)  
Kaya dadako na lang po ako sa susunod na katanungan ko kasi paulit-ulit na lang po yung tanong ko, paulit-ulit din po yung nagiging sagot.

**[07:45:40.260] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-007148)  
Sa indicator ng percentage increase ng bilang ng IPs assisted sa plan formulation, parehong 7% ang target para sa 2026 at 2027.

**[07:45:52.360] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-007149)  
Ano po ang dahilan kung bakit hindi tumataas ang target?

**[07:45:55.000] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-007150)  
At paano ito nakaka-apekto sa mga IP communities?

**[07:46:01.740] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007151)  
Madam Speaker, we have a universe of 999 ancestral domains.

**[07:46:10.920] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007152)  
Of these, 335 already have formulated ADSD PPS, leaving a gap of 664.

**[07:46:24.980] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007154)  
For fiscal year 2027, the funded target is only 30 ADSD PPS, equivalent to about 4.5% of the remaining gap.

**[07:46:38.720] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007155)  
After that, 634 would still remain.

**[07:46:44.020] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007156)  
This illustrates the mandate coverage gap.

**[07:46:47.640] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007157)  
Our annual target is determined by available resources while the actual need is much larger.

**[07:46:54.820] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007158)  
Maraming salamat po.

**[07:46:58.160] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-007159)  
Distinguished sponsor, can the NCIP confirm if it has a concrete plan to expedite the release of Certificate of Ancestral Domain title?

**[07:47:09.680] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-007160)  
Considering na mandato na ito ng komisyon mula pa noong na-enact ng IPRA, bakit tila nananatiling mabagal ang CAD-T issuance po natin?

**[07:47:21.560] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007161)  
Madam Speaker,

**[07:47:24.500] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007162)  
We are addressing not only the internal processing of CADTs and CALTs,

**[07:47:29.900] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007163)  
but also the interagency bottlenecks involving land records, registration, and overlapping claims.

**[07:47:38.400] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007164)  
NCIP has already met with the Land Registration Authority to streamline CADT/CALT registration and address overlaps and interagency requirements.

**[07:47:56.280] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007220)  
A technical working group was agreed upon to develop a clearer coordination mechanism.

**[07:48:03.060] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007221)  
We are also engaging in broader land justice initiatives, including the Supreme Court's Land and Justice Summit,

**[07:48:12.600] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007222)  
where systemic issues involving land disputes and overlapping claims can be addressed with the concerned institutions.

**[07:48:20.640] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007223)  
The Supreme Court had identified the Land and Justice Summit as one of its justice sector initiatives.

**[07:48:30.460] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007224)  
So, our approach is both operational and structural.

**[07:48:35.260] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007225)  
Expedite individual claims while addressing the policy fragmentation that causes recurring delays.

**[07:48:43.120] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007226)  
The Admin Order there on the contracting out-of-survey services in delineation

**[07:48:48.980] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007227)  
has been approved.

**[07:48:50.640] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007228)  
It is approved and is up for publication.

**[07:48:53.660] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007229)  
There is also an ongoing effort on digitization of our services and process

**[07:49:00.260] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007230)  
for more efficient processing, monitoring, and record-keeping.

**[07:49:07.420] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-007231)  
For this year po, sa 2026, saan po yung mga CAD-TIS na issue?

**[07:49:15.620] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-007232)

**[07:49:20.640] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007233)

**[07:49:27.860] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007234)

**[07:49:31.140] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007235)  
Region 9, meron pong tatlo.

**[07:49:34.100] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007236)  
Region 6, meron pong apat.

**[07:49:37.380] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007237)  
At Region 7, meron pong lima.

**[07:49:39.440] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007238)  
Or a total of 17 CAD-T in 2026.

**[07:49:48.160] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007239)  
As of July 31, 2026.

**[07:49:50.640] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-007240)  
And ongoing pa po ito.

**[07:49:52.800] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-007241)  
Good sponsor.

**[07:49:53.620] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-007242)  
Tama po?

**[07:49:56.200] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007243)  
Apo-apo.

**[07:49:57.400] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-007244)  
Maraming salamat po.

the CAD-T application involving

once the requirements

and funding arrangements

**[07:51:16.140] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007271)

**[07:51:17.280] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-007272)

**[07:51:23.020] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-007275)
