**[07:51:49.100] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-007286)  
Ano na po kaya ang status

**[07:51:51.100] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-007287)  
Ano na po kaya ang status

**[07:51:53.100] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-007288)  
ng revised FPIC guidelines?

**[07:51:55.100] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007289)  
Madam Speaker,

**[07:51:57.100] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007290)  
the revised FPIC guidelines

**[07:51:59.100] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007291)  
have already been approved

**[07:52:01.100] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007292)  
by the Commission en banc

**[07:52:05.100] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007294)  
and scheduled for publication.

**[07:52:07.100] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007295)  
This revision was not rushed.

**[07:52:11.100] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007297)  
The review started as early as 2016

**[07:52:13.100] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007298)  
and went through years of consultations,

**[07:52:17.100] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007300)  
reviews and deliberations,

**[07:52:19.100] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007301)  
involving ICCs,

**[07:52:21.100] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007302)  
IPs and other stakeholders.

**[07:52:25.100] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007304)  
We recognize the concerns being raised

**[07:52:27.100] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007305)  
because FPIC

**[07:52:29.100] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007306)  
is not merely

**[07:52:31.100] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007307)  
an administrative process.

**[07:52:33.100] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007308)  
It is a fundamental right

**[07:52:35.100] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007309)  
of ICCs, IPs

**[07:52:37.100] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007310)  
to decide

**[07:52:39.100] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007311)  
whether a project may proceed

**[07:52:41.100] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007312)  
and if they consent

**[07:52:43.100] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007313)  
to set the conditions,

**[07:52:45.100] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007314)  
limitations and safeguards

**[07:52:47.100] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007315)  
necessary to protect

**[07:52:49.100] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007316)  
their ancestral domains,

**[07:52:51.100] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007317)  
identity and culture.

**[07:52:53.100] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007318)  
Our objective

**[07:52:55.100] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007319)  
is to make government processing

**[07:52:57.100] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007320)  
more efficient and accountable

**[07:52:59.100] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007321)  
without weakening the

**[07:53:01.100] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007322)  
substance of FPIC.

**[07:53:03.100] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007323)  
Efficiency should never

**[07:53:05.100] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007324)  
replace genuine community

**[07:53:07.100] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007325)  
consent.

Unfortunately,

**[07:53:19.100] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-007330)

**[07:53:21.100] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-007331)

**[07:53:23.100] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-007332)

Unfortunately, sa mga

**[07:53:57.100] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-007349)

I will move to my

**[07:54:41.100] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-007371)

**[07:54:45.100] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-007373)

**[07:54:47.100] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-007374)  
Nananatiling

principal evidence ng consent

ng IPs,

**[07:54:53.100] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-007377)  
ICCs, ang memorandum

of agreement?

**[07:55:01.100] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007379)  
Opo, Madam Speaker,

**[07:55:03.100] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007380)  
the core requirement

**[07:55:05.100] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007381)  
remains the genuine

**[07:55:07.100] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007382)

**[07:55:09.100] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007383)  
ICCs, IPs,

**[07:55:11.100] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007384)  
through their own customary

**[07:55:13.100] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007385)  
decision-making process.

**[07:55:15.100] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007386)  
The MOA is retained

**[07:55:17.100] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007387)  
because consent

**[07:55:19.100] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007388)  
is not simply a

**[07:55:21.100] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007389)  
yes. The community

**[07:55:23.100] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007390)  
has the right to set conditions,

**[07:55:25.100] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007391)  
limitations,

**[07:55:27.100] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007392)  
benefits, and safeguards,

**[07:55:29.100] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007393)  
and these commitments must be

**[07:55:31.100] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007394)  
clearly documented

**[07:55:33.100] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007395)  
and enforceable.

**[07:55:35.100] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007396)  
In short, FPIC

**[07:55:37.100] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007397)  
protects both

**[07:55:39.100] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007398)  
the community's right to decide

**[07:55:41.100] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007399)  
and its right to define

**[07:55:43.100] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007400)  
the terms under which

**[07:55:45.100] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007401)  
it gives consent.

**[07:55:53.100] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-007405)  
NCIP elaborate

ng FPIC once

nag-commence po ito?

**[07:56:03.100] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-007409)  
Ano na po ang

**[07:56:05.100] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-007410)

**[07:56:07.100] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-007411)

**[07:56:13.100] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-007414)  
pag-ikli ng kabuo

**[07:56:15.100] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-007415)  
ang FPIC process.

**[07:56:45.100] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007416)

**[07:56:47.100] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007417)  
hindi po tama

**[07:56:49.100] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007418)  
na pinaikli ang

**[07:56:51.100] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007419)  
community deliberation.

**[07:56:53.100] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007420)  
In fact,

**[07:56:55.100] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007421)  
mas pinahaba po ito

**[07:56:57.100] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007422)  
under the 2026

**[07:56:59.100] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007423)  
FPIC guidelines.

**[07:57:01.100] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007424)  
The community may

**[07:57:03.100] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007425)  
have up to 60 working days

**[07:57:05.100] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007426)  
for consensus

**[07:57:07.100] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007427)  
building and decision-making,

**[07:57:09.100] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007428)  
compared with

**[07:57:11.100] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007429)  
30 days under the 2012

**[07:57:13.100] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007430)

**[07:57:15.100] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007431)  
43 calendar days

**[07:57:17.100] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007432)  
under E-BOS.

**[07:57:19.100] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007433)  
Mas malinaw din po

**[07:57:21.100] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007434)

**[07:57:23.100] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007435)

**[07:57:25.100] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007436)

**[07:57:27.100] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007437)

**[07:57:29.100] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007438)

**[07:57:33.100] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007440)

**[07:57:35.100] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007441)  
ng proponent.

**[07:57:37.100] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007442)  
Therefore, the time

**[07:57:39.100] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007443)

**[07:57:49.100] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007448)  
And there is no deemed consent.

**[07:57:51.100] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007449)  
Consent

**[07:57:53.100] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007450)  
must be affirmatively

**[07:57:55.100] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007451)

**[07:57:57.100] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007452)

**[07:57:59.100] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007453)

**[07:58:01.100] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007454)  
process.

the total timeline

50 working days

**[08:00:00.000] Rep. Ryan S. Recto** (asr-007508)  
Madam Speaker, I move to recognize the Honorable Jose Joboy S. Aquino II from the Lone District, City of Butuan.

**[08:00:10.960] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-007509)  
The Honorable Joboy Aquino from Butuan City is hereby recognized for his interpellation.

**[08:00:18.140] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-007510)  
Thank you, Madam Speaker, distinguished sponsor, my colleagues here in Congress, good afternoon.

**[08:00:27.740] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-007511)  
I would just like to make a manifestation, Madam Speaker.

**[08:00:34.760] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-007512)  
This representation would like to make a manifestation concerning the proposed budget of the National Commission on Indigenous People,

**[08:00:46.760] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-007513)  
given their glaring apathy and complete inaction during actual conflicts on the ground.

**[08:00:57.740] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-007514)  
Mr. Speaker, this year in Butuan City, an 18-year Good Friday tradition, the Panaat Pilgrimage in Barangay Antikala, was intentionally disrupted by political theater.

**[08:01:19.060] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-007515)  
Local indigenous groups were manipulated amid a clan power struggle.

**[08:01:27.740] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-007516)  
Dating back to 2020, weaponizing a sacred cultural observance for political gain.

**[08:01:37.900] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-007517)  
Rather than arguing in the media, my office initiated an immediate dialogue with local tribal leadership, barangay officials, the Philippine National Police, the Philippine Army, and the Red Cross,

**[08:01:56.160] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-007518)  
to have the...

**[08:01:57.740] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-007519)  
to have the resolution officially retracted.

**[08:02:01.340] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-007520)  
I must emphasize, Mr. Speaker, that this peaceful resolution was achieved completely without the intervention of the NCIP,

**[08:02:15.780] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-007521)  
who simply stood by and did nothing while tensions escalated.

**[08:02:25.800] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-007522)  
With this, I ask, does the...

**[08:02:27.740] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-007523)  
Does the NCIP have a standard, funded protocol for facilitating immediate dialogue in these instances?

**[08:02:39.220] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-007524)  
Or does it leave the responsibility entirely to the legislative and local government unit?

**[08:02:48.380] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-007525)  
Thank you.

**[08:02:54.460] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-007526)  
We allocate millions.

**[08:02:57.740] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-007527)  
To the Ancestral Domains Program to protect IP territories.

**[08:03:05.640] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-007528)  
But tensions frequently arise over the balance of ancestral claims with vested rights and public access to shared thoroughfares.

**[08:03:19.620] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-007529)  
Is there a dedicated budget allocation for a rapid response interagency

**[08:03:27.740] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-007530)  
mechanism?

**[08:03:28.140] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-007531)  
So the NCIP does not simply stand by and do nothing when these disputes erupt.

**[08:03:42.280] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-007532)  
Therefore, Mr. Speaker, I leave it to this floor, to the agency concerned,

**[08:03:49.380] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-007533)  
how they would understand and appreciate my sentiments on this issue.

**[08:03:57.740] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-007534)  
Sabi nga sa Bisaya, Madam Speaker, sa lugar namin, 'maulaw mo.' Sa mga Bisaya, naiintindihan ninyo 'yan. Kayo na mag-isip kung paano sa Tagalog 'yan. Thank you, Mr. Speaker and Mr. Sponsor.

**[08:04:21.700] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-007535)  
Majority Leader.

**[08:20:20.900] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-007801)

**[08:20:33.160] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-007804)  
itong February 2026, opisyal na napumirma ang Pilipinas sa Memorandum of Understanding

**[08:20:39.620] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-007805)  
on Critical Mineral Supply Chains, kung saan required ang mga bansa na mag-provide ng supply

**[08:20:46.880] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-007806)  
ng raw and processed critical minerals sa isa't isa, sa pamamagitan ng mining, exploration, at marami pang iba.

**[08:20:55.020] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-007807)

**[08:21:02.960] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-007808)

**[08:21:08.060] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007809)

**[08:21:16.880] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007810)  
within a week.

Ang FPIC po ay isang requirement.

**[08:21:44.860] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007812)  
sa mga

**[08:21:46.880] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007813)

**[08:21:49.960] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007814)

**[08:21:54.900] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-007815)

**[08:21:58.660] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-007816)

**[08:21:58.960] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-007817)

**[08:21:59.820] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007818)

**[08:22:01.100] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-007819)

**[08:22:05.840] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-007820)

**[08:22:27.800] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007824)  
Pag-aaralan po ng NCIP para po ma-improve.

**[08:22:32.760] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007825)  
Pero ang IPs po ang magdidesign.

**[08:22:37.080] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-007826)  
Yes.

**[08:22:37.860] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-007827)  
Opo, yan naman po ang nararapat.

**[08:22:40.620] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-007828)

**[08:22:46.880] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-007829)

**[08:22:48.720] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-007830)

**[08:22:52.700] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-007831)  
Pero hindi po, kumbaga, narinig yung boses ng most affected peoples, areas, and communities na ating mga indigenous peoples.

**[08:23:03.980] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-007832)  
Ngayon naman po, pagdating po sa pag-aaral na tungkol po sa situation ng pag-commit sa right to education.

**[08:23:16.880] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-007833)  
Quality and accessible education ng mga indigenous peoples.

**[08:23:21.660] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-007834)  
Meron na po ba kayong datos kung paano po matitiyak yung access sa education at all levels ng ating indigenous peoples?

**[08:23:34.740] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-007835)

**[08:23:39.280] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-007836)

**[08:23:42.680] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-007837)

**[08:23:46.360] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-007838)  
Para sa paggamit ng higher education ng mga students na IPs.

**[08:23:54.060] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-007839)  
Pwede po ba kayong magbahagi sa atin kung ano yung mga initiatives ng NCIP para mapunan ang education gap at masolusyonan ang inaccessibility pa rin ng learning at education sa ating bansa?

**[08:24:16.360] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007840)  
Sa PAMANA po, 1,372 beneficiaries.

**[08:24:30.900] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007841)

**[08:24:37.040] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007842)

Mr. Speaker, hingin po natin ang datos na yan, ang lahat ng mga detalye.

**[08:24:45.120] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-007844)  
Gayun din po.

**[08:24:45.740] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-007845)  
Pagdating sa trabaho, kabuhayan, pati na rin sa sektor ng agrikultura, mahalaga po yan kasi napapansin po natin sa maraming mga budget proposals at sa mga tinatalakay natin ngayong mga programa.

**[08:24:59.620] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-007846)  
Lagi pong naiiwan yung pagtitiyak na ang mga servisyo ay abot kamay din sa ating mga indigenous peoples.

**[08:25:08.920] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-007847)  
Kaya po, hingin po natin ang datos na yan at challenge, hindi lang po sa NCIP.

**[08:25:15.740] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-007848)  
Kapulungan na ito, natitiyakin sa lahat po ng oras ay hindi lang po included, pero matiyak natin, hindi lang yung inclusivity, pero dapat meron ding priority sa lahat po ng mga nananatili, na underserved ng mga communities dito sa ating bansa.

**[08:25:34.860] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-007849)

**[08:26:36.500] Rep. Arnan C. Panaligan** (asr-007853)  
Mr. Speaker, I move that we recognize the Honorable Antonio Tinio.

**[08:26:45.740] Rep. Arnan C. Panaligan** (asr-007854)  
of the Partylist Act Teachers to interpelate on the proposed budget of the NCIP.

**[08:26:54.220] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-007855)  
The Honorable Antonio Tino of Partylist Act Teachers is recognized. Please proceed.

**[08:27:19.810] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007856)  
Thank you, Mr. Speaker. Um,

**[08:27:25.900] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007858)  
Well, Mr. Speaker, tanong natin sa NCIP, ano po ang magiging involvement?

**[08:27:30.620] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007859)  
Or kasalukuyang involvement ng NCIP sa Paxilica?

**[08:27:53.900] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007860)  
Ang ginagawa po natin ay coordination po sa mga IPs para po,

**[08:28:00.620] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007861)  
nilang social safeguards.

**[08:28:03.840] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007862)  
Social safeguards. Ano pong ibig sabihin nun?

**[08:28:08.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007863)  
Well, ilinaw po natin bakit natin natanong ang Paxilica?

**[08:28:12.400] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007864)  
Dahil alam natin isang mahalagang component, malaking component ng Paxilica,

**[08:28:17.700] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007865)  
ayon na rin sa paliwanag ng gobyerno, ay yung critical minerals.

**[08:28:24.680] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007866)  
So, merong aspeto ng advanced manufacturing, sabi nila,

**[08:28:30.620] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007867)  
yung mga semiconductors, AI-related,

**[08:28:34.880] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007868)  
pero isang component din yung critical mineral supply chain.

**[08:28:40.740] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007869)  
At alam natin, ang ibig sabihin yan sa konteksto ng Pilipinas ay mining.

**[08:28:48.380] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007870)  
Tama po ba?

**[08:28:50.120] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007871)  
At alam naman natin, pag sinabing mining,

**[08:28:52.260] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007872)  
ay kadalasan,

**[08:28:55.340] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007873)  
masasaklaw o mahahagi po nito,

**[08:28:59.480] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007874)  
ang mga lupang ninuno.

**[08:29:02.040] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007875)  
Hindi po ba?

**[08:29:03.260] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007876)  
Kaya ang sinasabi nyo, ang papel ng NCIP ay pagtiyak ng,

**[08:29:09.660] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007877)  
ano yung sabi nyo kanina?

**[08:29:12.500] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007878)  
Ano po yung term na ginamit nyo?

**[08:29:16.900] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007879)  
Social safeguards po.

**[08:29:18.660] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007880)  
Social safeguards.

**[08:29:21.040] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007881)  
So, tama po bang intindi ko?

**[08:29:22.820] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007882)  
Ibig sabihin, kung yung lupain nila, lupang ninuno,

**[08:29:27.100] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007883)  
ay yan ang magiging target ng pagbimina NCIP.

**[08:29:33.520] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007884)  
Tutubo po ang magdi-desisyon kung papayagan o hindi ang proyekto.

**[08:29:39.120] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007885)  
Okay.

**[08:29:40.120] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007886)  
So, kaya nga po, napakahalaga nung free prior informed consent.

**[08:29:46.280] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007887)  
Tama po?

**[08:29:47.240] Rep. Rodolfo "Ompong" M. Ordanes** (asr-007888)  
Tama po.
